// ============================================
// VARIABLES GLOBALES
// ============================================
const APP_STORAGE_KEY = 'global_calculator_data_v16';
const BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY = 'calculator_backups';
const SETTINGS_KEY = 'calculator_settings_v6';
const RECENT_CALCULATIONS_KEY = 'recent_calculations';
const FUSION_STATES_KEY = 'fusion_states';
const SHORTCUTS_KEY = 'keyboard_shortcuts_v2';
const NOTES_STORAGE_KEY = 'multiline_notes_v4';
const EXCHANGE_RATES_KEY = 'exchange_rates_cache';
const TABLE_STORAGE_KEY = 'tableau_data_simple';
const CALC_STORAGE_KEY = 'quick_calculations';
const CALCULATOR_HISTORY_KEY = 'calculator_history_v2';

// Clés pour les sauvegardes par mode
const MODE_A_STATES_KEY = 'mode_a_saves';
const MODE_C_STATES_KEY = 'mode_c_saves';
const MODE_B_STATES_KEY = 'mode_b_saves';
const MODE_D_STATES_KEY = 'mode_d_saves';
const MODE_G_STATES_KEY = 'mode_g_saves';
const MODE_F_STATES_KEY = 'mode_f_saves';
const MODE_TABLE_STATES_KEY = 'mode_table_saves';

// Variables globales
let chartD = null, chartF = null;
let dDates = [], dSeries = [];
let historiqueF = [];
let fusionStates = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(FUSION_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
let conversionHistory = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('conversionHistory') || '[]');
let recentCalculations = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(RECENT_CALCULATIONS_KEY) || '[]');
let cBlocks = [];
let cBlockCounter = 1;
let exchangeRates = {};
let currentTheme = 'light';
let calculatorHistory = [];
let settings = {
    autoSave: true,
    notifications: true,
    confirmations: true,
    decimalPlaces: 2,
    defaultCurrency: 'EUR',
    autoRound: true,
    defaultTheme: 'light'
};
let keyboardShortcuts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SHORTCUTS_KEY) || '[]');

// Variables pour le mode Budget
let budgetCategories = [];
let budgetCatCount = 1;

// Variables pour le widget de notes
let notes = [];
let currentTab = 'audio';
let editingNoteId = null;
let recognition = null;
let isDictating = false;
let dictationFinalText = "";
let mediaRecorder = null;
let audioChunks = [];
let isRecording = false;
let pendingAudioBlob = null; // Stockage temporaire pour l'audio en cours

// Variables pour les sauvegardes par mode
let modeASaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_A_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
let modeCSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_C_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
let modeBSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_B_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
let modeDSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_D_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
let modeGSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_G_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
let modeFSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_F_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
let modeTableSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_TABLE_STATES_KEY) || '[]');

// Variables pour tableaux
let tables = [];
let currentTableId = null;
let tableCounter = 1;
let numberingType = 'numbers';
let currentMenuContext = { type: null, id: null, element: null };
let currentResizeColumn = null;
let currentResizeStartX = 0;
let currentResizeStartWidth = 0;
let dragSourceType = null;
let dragSourceId = null;
let dragSourceIndex = null;

// Variables pour la calculatrice scientifique
let calcHistory = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CALCULATOR_HISTORY_KEY) || '[]');

// État du menu latéral
let sidebarCollapsed = localStorage.getItem('sidebarCollapsed') === 'true';

// ============================================
// SAUVEGARDE GLOBALE AMÉLIORÉE
// ============================================

/**
 * Structure complète de la sauvegarde globale
 */
function collectAllAppData() {
    return {
        // Métadonnées
        version: '16.0',
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        appName: 'Calculateur Global Pro',
        
        // Notes et widget
        notes: notes || [],
        
        // Paramètres
        settings: settings || {},
        keyboardShortcuts: keyboardShortcuts || [],
        
        // Modes principaux
        calculator: {
            history: calcHistory || [],
            currentInput: document.getElementById('calc-input')?.value || ''
        },
        
        // Mode Tableaux
        tables: {
            list: tables || [],
            currentId: currentTableId,
            counter: tableCounter,
            numberingType: numberingType
        },
        
        // Mode C - Pourcentage
        modeC: {
            blocks: cBlocks || [],
            counter: cBlockCounter || 1
        },
        
        // Mode B - Budget
        modeB: {
            categories: budgetCategories || [],
            catCount: budgetCatCount || 1,
            globalBudget: document.getElementById('budget')?.value || '2000'
        },
        
        // Mode D - Évolution
        modeD: {
            dates: dDates || [],
            series: dSeries || [],
            useDates: document.getElementById('useDates')?.checked || true
        },
        
        // Mode F - Circulaire
        modeF: collectModeFData(),
        
        // Mode G - TVA
        modeG: collectModeGData(),
        
        // Mode H - Devises
        modeH: {
            conversionHistory: conversionHistory || [],
            lastFromCurrency: document.getElementById('fromCurrency')?.value || 'EUR',
            lastToCurrency: document.getElementById('toCurrency')?.value || 'USD'
        },
        
        // Mode A - Comparer
        modeA: collectModeAData(),
        
        // Calculs rapides
        quickCalculations: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CALC_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]'),
        
        // Statistiques et récents
        recentCalculations: recentCalculations || [],
        
        // États sauvegardés par mode
        savedStates: {
            modeA: modeASaves || [],
            modeB: modeBSaves || [],
            modeC: modeCSaves || [],
            modeD: modeDSaves || [],
            modeF: modeFSaves || [],
            modeG: modeGSaves || [],
            modeTable: modeTableSaves || []
        }
    };
}

/**
 * Collecte les données du Mode F
 */
function collectModeFData() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    return Array.from(blocks).map(block => ({
        label: block.querySelector('.labelF')?.value || '',
        value: block.querySelector('.valF')?.value || '0',
        color: block.querySelector('.colorF')?.value || '#3498db',
        originalValue: block.getAttribute('data-original-value'),
        isSelected: block.querySelector('.fusion-toggle-btn')?.getAttribute('data-selected') === 'true',
        isHidden: block.classList.contains('hidden-legend'),
        originalCategories: block.getAttribute('data-original-categories')
    }));
}

/**
 * Collecte les données du Mode A
 */
function collectModeAData() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiAContainer .ref-block');
    return Array.from(blocks).map(block => ({
        ref: block.querySelector('.refA')?.value || '100',
        vals: block.querySelector('.valsA')?.value || '80 120 100'
    }));
}

/**
 * Collecte les données du Mode G
 */
function collectModeGData() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiGContainer .ref-block');
    return Array.from(blocks).map(block => {
        const lines = Array.from(block.querySelectorAll('.ligne-tva')).map(line => ({
            id: line.id,
            libelle: line.querySelector('.libelleG')?.value || '',
            valeur: line.querySelector('.valeurG')?.value || '0',
            type: line.querySelector('.typeG')?.value || 'HT',
            tva: line.querySelector('.tvaIndividuelleG')?.value || '20'
        }));
        
        return {
            id: block.id,
            nom: block.querySelector('.bloc-nom-input')?.value || '',
            tvaGlobale: block.querySelector('.global-tva-input')?.value || '20',
            lignes: lines
        };
    });
}

/**
 * Affiche les statistiques des données
 */
function updateBackupStats() {
    const container = document.getElementById('backupStats');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const stats = [
        { label: 'Notes', count: notes?.length || 0, icon: 'sticky-note' },
        { label: 'Tableaux', count: tables?.length || 0, icon: 'table' },
        { label: 'Blocs %', count: cBlocks?.length || 0, icon: 'percentage' },
        { label: 'Catégories Budget', count: budgetCategories?.length || 0, icon: 'wallet' },
        { label: 'Séries Évolution', count: dSeries?.length || 0, icon: 'chart-line' },
        { label: 'Catégories Circulaire', count: document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block').length || 0, icon: 'chart-pie' },
        { label: 'Blocs TVA', count: document.querySelectorAll('#multiGContainer .ref-block').length || 0, icon: 'receipt' },
        { label: 'Conversions', count: conversionHistory?.length || 0, icon: 'exchange-alt' },
        { label: 'Raccourcis', count: keyboardShortcuts?.length || 0, icon: 'keyboard' }
    ];
    
    let html = '';
    stats.forEach(stat => {
        if (stat.count > 0) {
            html += `
                <div style="background: var(--bg-secondary); padding: 10px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid var(--border-light);">
                    <i class="fas fa-${stat.icon}" style="color: var(--accent-color); margin-right: 5px;"></i>
                    <span style="font-weight: bold;">${stat.count}</span>
                    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted); display: block;">${stat.label}</span>
                </div>
            `;
        }
    });
    
    if (html === '') {
        html = '<div style="grid-column: 1/-1; text-align: center; color: var(--text-muted); padding: 20px;">Aucune donnée à sauvegarder</div>';
    }
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

/**
 * Crée une sauvegarde globale complète
 */
function createGlobalBackup(backupName = null) {
    const appData = collectAllAppData();
    
    // Compter le nombre total d'éléments
    const totalItems = Object.values(appData).reduce((sum, val) => {
        if (Array.isArray(val)) return sum + val.length;
        if (val && typeof val === 'object') {
            return sum + Object.values(val).filter(Array.isArray).reduce((s, arr) => s + arr.length, 0);
        }
        return sum;
    }, 0);
    
    const defaultName = `Sauvegarde_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}_${new Date().toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')}`;
    const name = backupName || prompt('Nom de la sauvegarde globale :', defaultName);
    
    if (!name) return;
    
    const backup = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: name,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        data: appData,
        stats: {
            totalItems: totalItems,
            notes: appData.notes.length,
            tables: appData.tables.list.length,
            modes: Object.keys(appData.savedStates).length
        },
        size: JSON.stringify(appData).length
    };
    
    let backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    backups.unshift(backup);
    
    // Garder seulement les 20 dernières sauvegardes
    if (backups.length > 20) backups = backups.slice(0, 20);
    
    localStorage.setItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(backups));
    displayBackupList();
    updateBackupStats();
    showNotification(`✅ Sauvegarde globale "${name}" créée (${totalItems} éléments)`, 'success');
}

/**
 * Restaure une sauvegarde globale complète
 */
function restoreGlobalBackup(backupId) {
    const backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    const backup = backups.find(b => b.id === backupId);
    
    if (!backup) {
        showNotification('Sauvegarde introuvable', 'error');
        return;
    }
    
    const data = backup.data;
    const details = `
        📊 ${data.notes?.length || 0} notes
        📊 ${data.tables?.list?.length || 0} tableaux
        📊 ${data.modeC?.blocks?.length || 0} blocs %
        📊 ${data.modeB?.categories?.length || 0} catégories budget
        📊 ${data.modeD?.series?.length || 0} séries évolution
        📊 ${data.modeF?.length || 0} catégories circulaire
        📊 ${data.modeG?.length || 0} blocs TVA
    `;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Restaurer la sauvegarde "${backup.name}" ?\n${details}\n\nLes données actuelles seront écrasées.`)) {
        try {
            // Restaurer les notes
            if (data.notes) notes = data.notes;
            
            // Restaurer les paramètres
            if (data.settings) settings = { ...settings, ...data.settings };
            if (data.keyboardShortcuts) keyboardShortcuts = data.keyboardShortcuts;
            
            // Restaurer la calculatrice
            if (data.calculator) {
                calcHistory = data.calculator.history || [];
                const calcInput = document.getElementById('calc-input');
                if (calcInput && data.calculator.currentInput) {
                    calcInput.value = data.calculator.currentInput;
                }
            }
            
            // Restaurer les tableaux
            if (data.tables) {
                tables = data.tables.list || [];
                currentTableId = data.tables.currentId || null;
                tableCounter = data.tables.counter || 1;
                numberingType = data.tables.numberingType || 'numbers';
            }
            
            // Restaurer Mode C
            if (data.modeC) {
                cBlocks = data.modeC.blocks || [];
                cBlockCounter = data.modeC.counter || 1;
            }
            
            // Restaurer Mode B
            if (data.modeB) {
                budgetCategories = data.modeB.categories || [];
                budgetCatCount = data.modeB.catCount || 1;
                const budgetInput = document.getElementById('budget');
                if (budgetInput && data.modeB.globalBudget) {
                    budgetInput.value = data.modeB.globalBudget;
                }
            }
            
            // Restaurer Mode D
            if (data.modeD) {
                dDates = data.modeD.dates || [];
                dSeries = data.modeD.series || [];
                const useDates = document.getElementById('useDates');
                if (useDates && data.modeD.useDates !== undefined) {
                    useDates.checked = data.modeD.useDates;
                }
            }
            
            // Restaurer Mode F
            if (data.modeF) {
                restoreModeFData(data.modeF);
            }
            
            // Restaurer Mode G
            if (data.modeG) {
                restoreModeGData(data.modeG);
            }
            
            // Restaurer Mode H
            if (data.modeH) {
                conversionHistory = data.modeH.conversionHistory || [];
                const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency');
                const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency');
                if (fromCurrency && data.modeH.lastFromCurrency) {
                    fromCurrency.value = data.modeH.lastFromCurrency;
                }
                if (toCurrency && data.modeH.lastToCurrency) {
                    toCurrency.value = data.modeH.lastToCurrency;
                }
            }
            
            // Restaurer Mode A
            if (data.modeA) {
                restoreModeAData(data.modeA);
            }
            
            // Restaurer les calculs rapides
            if (data.quickCalculations) {
                localStorage.setItem(CALC_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data.quickCalculations));
            }
            
            // Restaurer les calculs récents
            if (data.recentCalculations) {
                recentCalculations = data.recentCalculations;
            }
            
            // Restaurer les états sauvegardés
            if (data.savedStates) {
                modeASaves = data.savedStates.modeA || [];
                modeBSaves = data.savedStates.modeB || [];
                modeCSaves = data.savedStates.modeC || [];
                modeDSaves = data.savedStates.modeD || [];
                modeFSaves = data.savedStates.modeF || [];
                modeGSaves = data.savedStates.modeG || [];
                modeTableSaves = data.savedStates.modeTable || [];
            }
            
            // Sauvegarder tout immédiatement
            saveAllData();
            saveNotes();
            
            // Rafraîchir toutes les interfaces
            refreshAllInterfaces();
            
            showNotification(`✅ Sauvegarde "${backup.name}" restaurée avec succès`, 'success');
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erreur restauration:', error);
            showNotification('❌ Erreur lors de la restauration', 'error');
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Restaure les données du Mode F
 */
function restoreModeFData(blocksData) {
    const container = document.getElementById('multiFContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    
    container.innerHTML = '';
    
    blocksData.forEach(blockData => {
        const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.className = 'ref-block';
        div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
        
        if (blockData.originalValue) div.setAttribute('data-original-value', blockData.originalValue);
        if (blockData.originalCategories) div.setAttribute('data-original-categories', blockData.originalCategories);
        if (blockData.isHidden) div.classList.add('hidden-legend');
        
        div.innerHTML = `
            <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="${blockData.label}" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="${blockData.value}" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                <input type="color" class="colorF" value="${blockData.color}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="${blockData.isSelected}" style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none; background: ${blockData.isSelected ? 'var(--success-color)' : 'var(--bg-tertiary)'}; color: ${blockData.isSelected ? 'white' : 'var(--text-primary)'};">${blockData.isSelected ? '✓' : '✗'}</div>
                <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
            </div>
        `;
        
        container.appendChild(div);
    });
}

/**
 * Restaure les données du Mode G
 */
function restoreModeGData(blocksData) {
    const container = document.getElementById('multiGContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    
    container.innerHTML = '';
    
    blocksData.forEach(blockData => {
        const blockId = blockData.id || 'gblock-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.className = 'ref-block';
        div.id = blockId;
        
        let linesHTML = '';
        blockData.lignes.forEach(line => {
            const ligneId = line.id || 'ligne-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
            linesHTML += `
                <div class="ligne-tva" id="${ligneId}" style="display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:center; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                    <input type="text" class="libelleG" placeholder="Libellé" value="${line.libelle}" style="flex:1; min-width: 150px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                    <input type="number" class="valeurG" placeholder="Montant" value="${line.valeur}" style="width:110px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                    <select class="typeG" style="width:90px;" onchange="autoCalculG()">
                        <option value="HT" ${line.type === 'HT' ? 'selected' : ''}>HT</option>
                        <option value="TTC" ${line.type === 'TTC' ? 'selected' : ''}>TTC</option>
                    </select>
                    <input type="number" class="tvaIndividuelleG" placeholder="TVA (%)" value="${line.tva}" style="width:80px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                    <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn" style="padding: 5px 10px;"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>
                </div>
            `;
        });
        
        div.innerHTML = `
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; width:100%; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                <input type="text" class="bloc-nom-input" value="${blockData.nom}" placeholder="Nom du bloc" style="flex:1; min-width: 200px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                <label style="color: var(--text-primary); white-space: nowrap;">TVA Globale (%): <input type="number" class="global-tva-input" value="${blockData.tvaGlobale}" style="width:70px;" oninput="autoCalculG()"></label>
                <button onclick="addGLigneToBlock('${blockId}')" class="secondary"><i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Ligne</button>
                <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
            </div>
            <div class="ligneGContainer" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:10px; margin-top:15px;">
                ${linesHTML}
            </div>
        `;
        
        container.appendChild(div);
    });
}

/**
 * Rafraîchit toutes les interfaces après restauration
 */
function refreshAllInterfaces() {
    // Rafraîchir les calculs
    updateCalcHistoryDisplay();
    updatePreview();
    
    // Rafraîchir les tableaux
    renderTableTabs();
    renderCurrentTable();
    
    // Rafraîchir les modes
    renderCBlocks();
    updateCResults();
    renderBudgetCategories();
    updateBudgetRecap();
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    autoCalculF();
    calculerF();
    calculerG();
    autoCalculA();
    
    // Rafraîchir les listes de sauvegardes
    displayModeASaves();
    displayModeBSaves();
    displayModeCSaves();
    displayModeDSaves();
    displayModeFSaves();
    displayModeGSaves();
    displayTableSavesList();
    
    // Rafraîchir les devises
    if (typeof convertCurrency === 'function') {
        updateFlags();
        convertCurrency();
    }
    
    // Rafraîchir les notes
    renderNotes();
    
    // Rafraîchir le dashboard
    updateDashboard();
    
    // Appliquer les paramètres
    loadSettings();
    
    // Réactiver les inputs améliorés
    enhanceNumberInputs();
    
    // Sauvegarder l'état
    saveAllData();
    saveNotes();
}

/**
 * Exporte la sauvegarde courante vers un fichier
 */
function exportGlobalBackup() {
    const backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    
    if (backups.length === 0) {
        showNotification('Aucune sauvegarde à exporter', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    // Demander quelle sauvegarde exporter
    const backupNames = backups.map((b, index) => `${index + 1}. ${b.name} (${new Date(b.timestamp).toLocaleDateString()})`).join('\n');
    const choice = prompt(`Quelle sauvegarde exporter ? (Entrez le numéro)\n\n${backupNames}`, '1');
    
    if (!choice) return;
    
    const index = parseInt(choice) - 1;
    if (index >= 0 && index < backups.length) {
        const backup = backups[index];
        
        const dataStr = JSON.stringify(backup, null, 2);
        const dataUri = 'data:application/json;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(dataStr);
        const fileName = `calculateur_${backup.name.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, '_')}_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
        
        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = dataUri;
        link.download = fileName;
        link.click();
        
        showNotification(`Sauvegarde "${backup.name}" exportée`, 'success');
    } else {
        showNotification('Numéro invalide', 'error');
    }
}

/**
 * Importe une sauvegarde depuis un fichier
 */
function importGlobalBackupFile() {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = '.json';
    
    input.onchange = function(event) {
        const file = event.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = function(e) {
            try {
                const importedData = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
                
                // Vérifier si c'est une sauvegarde complète ou une ancienne sauvegarde
                if (importedData.data && importedData.name && importedData.timestamp) {
                    // C'est une sauvegarde complète
                    if (confirmWithSetting(`Importer la sauvegarde "${importedData.name}" ?`)) {
                        let backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
                        backups.unshift(importedData);
                        localStorage.setItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(backups));
                        displayBackupList();
                        showNotification('Sauvegarde importée avec succès', 'success');
                    }
                } else if (importedData.notes || importedData.tables || importedData.modeC) {
                    // C'est une sauvegarde de données directe, la convertir en sauvegarde complète
                    const newBackup = {
                        id: Date.now(),
                        name: `Import_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`,
                        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
                        data: importedData,
                        stats: {
                            totalItems: Object.keys(importedData).length,
                            notes: importedData.notes?.length || 0,
                            tables: importedData.tables?.list?.length || 0
                        },
                        size: JSON.stringify(importedData).length
                    };
                    
                    if (confirmWithSetting(`Convertir et importer ces données ?`)) {
                        let backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
                        backups.unshift(newBackup);
                        localStorage.setItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(backups));
                        displayBackupList();
                        showNotification('Données importées et converties en sauvegarde', 'success');
                    }
                } else {
                    showNotification('Format de fichier non reconnu', 'error');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erreur import:', error);
                showNotification('Erreur lors de l\'importation', 'error');
            }
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
    };
    
    input.click();
}

/**
 * Affiche la liste des sauvegardes avec plus de détails
 */
function displayBackupList() {
    const container = document.getElementById('backupList');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    
    if (backups.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px 20px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <i class="fas fa-inbox fa-2x" style="margin-bottom: 15px;"></i>
                <p>Aucune sauvegarde disponible</p>
                <p style="font-size: 12px;">Créez votre première sauvegarde globale</p>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    backups.forEach(backup => {
        const date = new Date(backup.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
        const size = (backup.size / 1024).toFixed(2);
        const items = backup.stats?.totalItems || '?';
        
        html += `
            <div style="background: var(--bg-secondary); border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 8px; padding: 15px; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div>
                        <div style="font-weight: 600; font-size: 16px; margin-bottom: 5px;">
                            <i class="fas fa-database" style="color: var(--accent-color); margin-right: 5px;"></i>
                            ${backup.name}
                        </div>
                        <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                            ${date} • ${size} Ko • ${items} éléments
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 5px;">
                        <button onclick="restoreGlobalBackup(${backup.id})" class="secondary" style="padding: 8px 12px;" title="Restaurer">
                            <i class="fas fa-undo"></i>
                        </button>
                        <button onclick="exportSingleBackup(${backup.id})" class="secondary" style="padding: 8px 12px;" title="Exporter">
                            <i class="fas fa-download"></i>
                        </button>
                        <button onclick="deleteBackup(${backup.id})" class="delete-btn" style="padding: 8px 12px;" title="Supprimer">
                            <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                <!-- Aperçu rapide du contenu -->
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 5px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 10px; font-size: 11px;">
                    ${backup.stats?.notes ? `<span style="background: var(--bg-tertiary); padding: 3px 8px; border-radius: 12px;"><i class="fas fa-sticky-note"></i> ${backup.stats.notes}</span>` : ''}
                    ${backup.stats?.tables ? `<span style="background: var(--bg-tertiary); padding: 3px 8px; border-radius: 12px;"><i class="fas fa-table"></i> ${backup.stats.tables}</span>` : ''}
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
    updateBackupStats();
}

/**
 * Exporte une sauvegarde spécifique
 */
function exportSingleBackup(backupId) {
    const backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    const backup = backups.find(b => b.id === backupId);
    
    if (!backup) {
        showNotification('Sauvegarde introuvable', 'error');
        return;
    }
    
    const dataStr = JSON.stringify(backup, null, 2);
    const dataUri = 'data:application/json;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(dataStr);
    const fileName = `calculateur_${backup.name.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, '_')}_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
    
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = dataUri;
    link.download = fileName;
    link.click();
    
    showNotification(`Sauvegarde "${backup.name}" exportée`, 'success');
}

/**
 * Supprime une sauvegarde
 */
function deleteBackup(backupId) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette sauvegarde ?')) {
        const backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
        const updatedBackups = backups.filter(b => b.id !== backupId);
        localStorage.setItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(updatedBackups));
        displayBackupList();
        showNotification('Sauvegarde supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

/**
 * Supprime toutes les sauvegardes
 */
function clearAllBackups() {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer TOUTES les sauvegardes ? Cette action est irréversible.')) {
        localStorage.removeItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY);
        displayBackupList();
        showNotification('Toutes les sauvegardes ont été supprimées', 'success');
    }
}

/**
 * Gère l'import d'un fichier de sauvegarde
 */
function handleBackupFileSelect(event) {
    const file = event.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = function(e) {
        try {
            const importedData = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
            
            if (importedData.data && importedData.name && importedData.timestamp) {
                if (confirmWithSetting(`Importer la sauvegarde "${importedData.name}" ?`)) {
                    let backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
                    backups.unshift(importedData);
                    localStorage.setItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(backups));
                    displayBackupList();
                    showNotification('Sauvegarde importée avec succès', 'success');
                }
            } else {
                showNotification('Format de fichier non reconnu', 'error');
            }
        } catch (error) {
            showNotification('Erreur lors de l\'importation', 'error');
        }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS DU MENU LATÉRAL
// ============================================
function toggleSidebar() {
    sidebarCollapsed = !sidebarCollapsed;
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const toggleBtn = document.querySelector('.toggle-btn i');
    
    if (sidebar) {
        if (sidebarCollapsed) {
            sidebar.classList.add('collapsed');
            if (toggleBtn) toggleBtn.className = 'fas fa-chevron-right';
        } else {
            sidebar.classList.remove('collapsed');
            if (toggleBtn) toggleBtn.className = 'fas fa-chevron-left';
        }
    }
    
    localStorage.setItem('sidebarCollapsed', sidebarCollapsed);
}

function setActiveMenuItem(element) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(item => {
        item.classList.remove('active');
    });
    element.classList.add('active');
    const modeTitle = element.querySelector('span')?.textContent || 'Accueil';
    const titleEl = document.getElementById('currentModeTitle');
    if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = modeTitle;
}

// ============================================
// FONCTION PRINCIPALE D'AFFICHAGE DES MODES
// ============================================
function showMode(modeId) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.mode-content').forEach(el => {
        el.classList.add('hidden');
    });
    
    if (modeId !== 'none' && document.getElementById(modeId)) {
        document.getElementById(modeId).classList.remove('hidden');
        
        const menuItems = document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item');
        let found = false;
        
        menuItems.forEach(item => {
            const onclick = item.getAttribute('onclick');
            if (onclick && onclick.includes(modeId)) {
                item.classList.add('active');
                const modeTitle = item.querySelector('span')?.textContent || 'Accueil';
                const titleEl = document.getElementById('currentModeTitle');
                if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = modeTitle;
                found = true;
            } else {
                item.classList.remove('active');
            }
        });
        
        if (!found && modeId === 'home') {
            const homeItem = document.querySelector('.menu-item[onclick*="home"]');
            if (homeItem) homeItem.classList.add('active');
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('currentModeTitle');
            if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = 'Accueil';
        }
        
        // Initialisations spécifiques avec affichage automatique
        if (modeId === 'modeC') {
            if (cBlocks.length === 0) addCBlock();
            else {
                renderCBlocks();
                updateCResults();
            }
        }
        if (modeId === 'modeH') {
            loadExchangeRates();
            displayModeHSaves();
        }
        if (modeId === 'modeF') {
            autoCalculF();
            displayModeFSaves();
        }
        if (modeId === 'modeD') {
            updateDTable();
            calculerD();
            displayModeDSaves();
        }
        if (modeId === 'home') updateDashboard();
        if (modeId === 'modeB') {
            if (budgetCategories.length === 0) addBudgetCategory();
            else {
                renderBudgetCategories();
                updateBudgetRecap();
            }
            displayModeBSaves();
        }
        if (modeId === 'modeA') {
            if (document.querySelectorAll('#multiAContainer .ref-block').length === 0) addABlock();
            else autoCalculA();
            displayModeASaves();
        }
        if (modeId === 'modeG') {
            if (document.querySelectorAll('#multiGContainer .ref-block').length === 0) addGBlock();
            else calculerG();
            displayModeGSaves();
        }
        if (modeId === 'modeTableau') {
            renderTableTabs();
            if (currentTableId) renderCurrentTable();
            displayTableSavesList();
        }
        if (modeId === 'modeCalculs') updateCalculations();
        
        const notesPanel = document.getElementById('notesPanel');
        if (notesPanel) notesPanel.classList.remove('active');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

}

}

    displaySavesList(modeDSaves, 'modeDSavesList', 'restoreModeDState', 'deleteModeDSave');
}

    displaySavesList(modeFSaves, 'modeFSavesList', 'restoreModeFState', 'deleteModeFSave');
}

    displaySavesList(modeGSaves, 'modeGSavesList', 'restoreModeGState', 'deleteModeGSave');

}

function displaySavesList(saves, containerId, restoreFunction, deleteFunction) {
    const container = document.getElementById(containerId);
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (saves.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Aucune sauvegarde</div>';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    saves.forEach(save => {
        const date = new Date(save.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
        html += `
            <div class="save-item">
                <div class="save-info">
                    <div class="save-name">${save.name}</div>
                    <div class="save-date">${date}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="save-actions">
                    <button onclick="${restoreFunction}(${save.id})" class="secondary save-btn-small">
                        <i class="fas fa-undo"></i>
                    </button>
                    <button onclick="${deleteFunction}(${save.id})" class="delete-btn save-btn-small">
                        <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS D'AMÉLIORATION DES INPUTS
// ============================================
function enhanceNumberInputs() {
    const numberInputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[type="number"], .budget-input, .percentage-input, .valF, .valeurG, .refA, .amount-input, .table-cell-input[type="number"]');
    
    numberInputs.forEach(input => {
        if (input.dataset.wheelEnhanced === 'true') return;
        input.dataset.wheelEnhanced = 'true';
        
        input.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            
            let step = 1;
            if (e.shiftKey) step = 10;
            else if (e.ctrlKey) step = 0.1;
            
            const delta = e.deltaY < 0 ? step : -step;
            
            let currentVal = parseFloat(this.value);
            if (isNaN(currentVal)) currentVal = 0;
            
            this.value = (currentVal + delta).toFixed(e.ctrlKey ? 1 : 0);
            
            this.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
            this.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        }, { passive: false });
    });
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS DU WIDGET NOTES AVEC STOCKAGE TEMPORAIRE ROBUSTE
// ============================================
function loadNotes() {
    try {
        const savedNotes = localStorage.getItem(NOTES_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (savedNotes) {
            notes = JSON.parse(savedNotes);
            if (!Array.isArray(notes)) notes = [];
            
            notes = notes.map(note => {
                if (note.type === 'audio' && note.content && !note.content.startsWith('data:audio')) {
                    note.content = note.content || '';
                }
                return note;
            });
        } else {
            notes = [];
        }
        console.log('📝 Notes chargées:', notes.length);
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Erreur chargement notes:', e);
        notes = [];
    }
    renderNotes();
}

function saveNotes() {
    try {
        localStorage.setItem(NOTES_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(notes));
        console.log('✅ Notes sauvegardées:', notes.length);
        renderNotes();
        saveAllData();
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Erreur sauvegarde notes:', e);
        showNotification('Erreur lors de la sauvegarde des notes', 'error');
    }
}

// Fonction pour sauvegarder l'audio en cours avant changement de mode
function savePendingAudio() {
    if (pendingAudioBlob) {
        console.log('🎵 Sauvegarde de l\'audio en cours avant changement de mode...');
        
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onloadend = () => {
            const newNote = {
                id: Date.now(),
                type: 'audio',
                content: reader.result,
                date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
                name: "Audio " + new Date().toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')
            };
            
            notes.unshift(newNote);
            saveNotes();
            pendingAudioBlob = null;
            
            if (currentTab !== 'audio') {
                switchTab('audio');
            } else {
                renderNotes();
            }
            
            showNotification('Enregistrement audio sauvegardé automatiquement', 'success');
        };
        
        reader.readAsDataURL(pendingAudioBlob);
        
        // Réinitialiser l'état d'enregistrement
        isRecording = false;
        const btnRecord = document.getElementById('btnRecord');
        if (btnRecord) {
            btnRecord.classList.remove('btn-stop');
            btnRecord.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-microphone"></i> Audio';
        }
    }
}

function toggleNotesPanel() {
    const panel = document.getElementById('notesPanel');
    if (panel) {
        panel.classList.toggle('active');
        if (panel.classList.contains('active')) {
            renderNotes();
        }
    }
}

function switchTab(tab) {
    currentTab = tab;
    const tabAudio = document.getElementById('tabAudio');
    const tabText = document.getElementById('tabText');
    
    if (tabAudio) tabAudio.classList.toggle('active', tab === 'audio');
    if (tabText) tabText.classList.toggle('active', tab === 'text');
    
    cancelEdit();
    renderNotes();
}

function renderNotes() {
    const container = document.getElementById('notesList');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const filtered = notes.filter(n => n && n.type === currentTab);
    
    if (filtered.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; margin-top:50px; opacity:0.5; color: var(--widget-text);">Aucune note</div>';
        return;
    }

    let html = '';
    filtered.forEach(n => {
        if (!n || !n.id) return;
        
        let contentHTML = "";
        const noteId = n.id;
        const noteName = n.name || n.date || 'Sans titre';
        
        if (n.subType === 'checklist') {
            contentHTML = `<div class="checklist-container">`;
            if (n.items && n.items.length > 0) {
                n.items.forEach((item, idx) => {
                    if (!item) return;
                    contentHTML += `
                        <div class="check-item ${item.checked ? 'done' : ''}">
                            <input type="checkbox" ${item.checked ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleCheckItem(${noteId}, ${idx})">
                            <span ondblclick="editCheckItem(${noteId}, ${idx})">${item.text || ''}</span>
                            <i class="fas fa-times" onclick="removeCheckItem(${noteId}, ${idx})" style="margin-left:auto; opacity:0.5; cursor:pointer;"></i>
                        </div>
                    `;
                });
            }
            contentHTML += `
                    <input type="text" class="add-check-input" placeholder="+ Ajouter..." onkeypress="handleCheckEntry(event, ${noteId})">
                </div>`;
        } else if (n.type === 'text') {
            contentHTML = `<div class="text-content" ondblclick="openEditor(${noteId})">${n.content || 'Note vide...'}</div>`;
        } else if (n.type === 'audio') {
            if (n.content && n.content.startsWith('data:audio')) {
                contentHTML = `
                    <audio controls src="${n.content}" style="width:100%; margin-bottom: 5px;"></audio>
                    <div style="font-size:0.8em; margin-top:5px; opacity:0.7;">
                        <i class="fas fa-microphone"></i> Enregistrement audio
                    </div>`;
            } else {
                contentHTML = `
                    <div style="padding: 10px; background: rgba(255,0,0,0.1); border-radius: 4px;">
                        <i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Audio non disponible
                    </div>`;
            }
        }

        html += `
            <div class="note-item" data-note-id="${noteId}">
                <div class="note-header">
                    <div class="note-title" ondblclick="renameNote(${noteId})">
                        <i class="fas fa-pen-nib" style="font-size:0.8em; opacity:0.6"></i> ${noteName}
                    </div>
                    <i class="fas fa-trash" onclick="deleteNote(${noteId})" style="color:var(--error-color); cursor:pointer;"></i>
                </div>
                ${contentHTML}
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function openEditor(id = null) {
    editingNoteId = id;
    const editor = document.getElementById('textEditorUI');
    const list = document.getElementById('notesList');
    const titleInput = document.getElementById('editTitle');
    const textInput = document.getElementById('editText');

    if (!editor || !list || !titleInput || !textInput) return;

    if (id) {
        const note = notes.find(n => n.id === id);
        if (note) {
            titleInput.value = note.name || "";
            textInput.value = note.content || "";
        }
    } else {
        titleInput.value = "Nouvelle Note";
        textInput.value = "";
    }

    list.style.display = 'none';
    editor.style.display = 'flex';
    textInput.focus();
}

function cancelEdit() {
    const editor = document.getElementById('textEditorUI');
    const list = document.getElementById('notesList');
    if (editor) editor.style.display = 'none';
    if (list) list.style.display = 'block';
    editingNoteId = null;
}

function saveTextNote() {
    const titleInput = document.getElementById('editTitle');
    const textInput = document.getElementById('editText');
    
    if (!titleInput || !textInput) return;
    
    const title = titleInput.value || "Sans titre";
    const content = textInput.value || "";

    if (editingNoteId) {
        const note = notes.find(n => n.id === editingNoteId);
        if (note) {
            note.name = title;
            note.content = content;
        }
    } else {
        notes.unshift({
            id: Date.now(),
            type: 'text',
            subType: 'free',
            name: title,
            content: content,
            date: new Date().toLocaleString()
        });
    }

    saveNotes();
    cancelEdit();
    switchTab('text');
}

function createNewChecklist() {
    const name = prompt("Nom de la liste :");
    if (!name) return;
    
    notes.unshift({
        id: Date.now(),
        type: 'text',
        subType: 'checklist',
        name: name,
        date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
        items: []
    });
    
    saveNotes();
    switchTab('text');
}

function handleCheckEntry(e, noteId) {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target.value.trim()) {
        const note = notes.find(n => n.id === noteId);
        if (note) {
            if (!note.items) note.items = [];
            note.items.push({ 
                text: e.target.value.trim(), 
                checked: false 
            });
            e.target.value = "";
            saveNotes();
        }
    }
}

function toggleCheckItem(noteId, itemIndex) {
    const note = notes.find(n => n.id === noteId);
    if (note && note.items && note.items[itemIndex]) {
        note.items[itemIndex].checked = !note.items[itemIndex].checked;
        saveNotes();
    }
}

function removeCheckItem(noteId, itemIndex) {
    const note = notes.find(n => n.id === noteId);
    if (note && note.items) {
        note.items.splice(itemIndex, 1);
        saveNotes();
    }
}

function editCheckItem(noteId, itemIndex) {
    const note = notes.find(n => n.id === noteId);
    if (note && note.items && note.items[itemIndex]) {
        const newText = prompt("Modifier l'élément :", note.items[itemIndex].text);
        if (newText !== null && newText.trim() !== '') {
            note.items[itemIndex].text = newText.trim();
            saveNotes();
        }
    }
}

function deleteNote(id) {
    if (confirmWithSetting("Supprimer cette note ?")) {
        notes = notes.filter(n => n.id !== id);
        saveNotes();
        showNotification('Note supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

function renameNote(id) {
    const note = notes.find(n => n.id === id);
    if (note) {
        const newName = prompt("Renommer la note :", note.name || note.date);
        if (newName && newName.trim() !== '') {
            note.name = newName.trim();
            saveNotes();
            showNotification('Note renommée', 'success');
        }
    }
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS AUDIO AVEC STOCKAGE TEMPORAIRE
// ============================================
async function toggleAudio() {
    const btnRecord = document.getElementById('btnRecord');
    if (!btnRecord) return;
    
    if (isRecording) {
        if (mediaRecorder && mediaRecorder.state !== 'inactive') {
            mediaRecorder.stop();
        }
        isRecording = false;
        btnRecord.classList.remove('btn-stop');
        btnRecord.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-microphone"></i> Audio';
        showNotification('Enregistrement terminé...', 'info');
        
    } else {
        try {
            const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
            
            audioChunks = [];
            
            const mimeTypes = [
                'audio/webm;codecs=opus',
                'audio/webm',
                'audio/ogg;codecs=opus',
                'audio/mp4',
                'audio/mpeg'
            ];
            
            let options = {};
            for (let type of mimeTypes) {
                if (MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(type)) {
                    options = { mimeType: type };
                    break;
                }
            }
            
            mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, options);
            
            mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {
                if (e.data && e.data.size > 0) {
                    audioChunks.push(e.data);
                }
            };
            
            mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
                if (audioChunks.length === 0) {
                    showNotification('Enregistrement vide', 'warning');
                    stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
                    return;
                }
                
                const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunks, { 
                    type: options.mimeType || 'audio/webm' 
                });
                
                // Sauvegarder dans pendingAudioBlob pour garantir la persistance
                pendingAudioBlob = audioBlob;
                
                // Sauvegarder immédiatement
                savePendingAudio();
                
                audioChunks = [];
                stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
            };
            
            mediaRecorder.onerror = (e) => {
                console.error('Erreur enregistrement:', e);
                showNotification('Erreur d\'enregistrement', 'error');
                isRecording = false;
                btnRecord.classList.remove('btn-stop');
                btnRecord.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-microphone"></i> Audio';
                stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
            };
            
            mediaRecorder.start();
            isRecording = true;
            
            btnRecord.classList.add('btn-stop');
            btnRecord.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-stop"></i> Arrêter';
            
            switchTab('audio');
            showNotification('Enregistrement démarré', 'success');
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erreur microphone:', error);
            
            if (error.name === 'NotAllowedError') {
                showNotification('Permission microphone refusée', 'error');
            } else if (error.name === 'NotFoundError') {
                showNotification('Microphone non trouvé', 'error');
            } else {
                showNotification('Erreur microphone: ' + error.message, 'error');
            }
            
            isRecording = false;
            btnRecord.classList.remove('btn-stop');
            btnRecord.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-microphone"></i> Audio';
        }
    }
}

function toggleDictation() {
    const btnDictate = document.getElementById('btnDictate');
    if (!btnDictate) return;
    
    if (!('webkitSpeechRecognition' in window) && !('SpeechRecognition' in window)) {
        showNotification('Reconnaissance vocale non supportée', 'error');
        return;
    }
    
    if (isDictating) {
        if (recognition) {
            recognition.stop();
        }
        
        if (dictationFinalText.trim()) {
            notes.unshift({
                id: Date.now(),
                type: 'text',
                subType: 'free',
                content: dictationFinalText.trim(),
                date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
                name: "Dictée " + new Date().toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')
            });
            
            saveNotes();
            showNotification('Dictée enregistrée', 'success');
            dictationFinalText = "";
            
            switchTab('text');
        }
        
        isDictating = false;
        btnDictate.classList.remove('btn-stop');
        btnDictate.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-keyboard"></i> Dictée';
        const dictationUI = document.getElementById('dictationUI');
        if (dictationUI) dictationUI.style.display = 'none';
        
    } else {
        try {
            const SpeechRecognition = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
            recognition = new SpeechRecognition();
            recognition.lang = 'fr-FR';
            recognition.continuous = true;
            recognition.interimResults = true;
            recognition.maxAlternatives = 1;
            
            recognition.onstart = () => {
                console.log('Dictée démarrée');
            };
            
            recognition.onresult = (event) => {
                let interimTranscript = '';
                let finalTranscript = '';
                
                for (let i = event.resultIndex; i < event.results.length; i++) {
                    const transcript = event.results[i][0].transcript;
                    if (event.results[i].isFinal) {
                        finalTranscript += transcript + ' ';
                    } else {
                        interimTranscript += transcript;
                    }
                }
                
                if (finalTranscript) {
                    dictationFinalText += finalTranscript;
                }
                
                const interimEl = document.getElementById('interimText');
                if (interimEl) {
                    interimEl.innerText = dictationFinalText + interimTranscript;
                }
            };
            
            recognition.onerror = (event) => {
                console.error('Erreur dictée:', event.error);
                if (event.error === 'not-allowed') {
                    showNotification('Microphone non autorisé', 'error');
                } else if (event.error === 'no-speech') {
                    showNotification('Aucune parole détectée', 'warning');
                }
                isDictating = false;
                btnDictate.classList.remove('btn-stop');
                btnDictate.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-keyboard"></i> Dictée';
                const dictationUI = document.getElementById('dictationUI');
                if (dictationUI) dictationUI.style.display = 'none';
            };
            
            recognition.onend = () => {
                if (isDictating) {
                    try {
                        recognition.start();
                    } catch (e) {
                        console.log('Erreur redémarrage:', e);
                    }
                }
            };
            
            dictationFinalText = "";
            recognition.start();
            isDictating = true;
            
            btnDictate.classList.add('btn-stop');
            btnDictate.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-microphone-slash"></i> Arrêter';
            const dictationUI = document.getElementById('dictationUI');
            if (dictationUI) dictationUI.style.display = 'block';
            
            showNotification('Dictée démarrée - Parlez', 'info');
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erreur démarrage dictée:', error);
            showNotification('Erreur dictée: ' + error.message, 'error');
        }
    }
}

// ============================================
// CALCULATRICE SCIENTIFIQUE - NOUVELLE VERSION
// ============================================
function insertSymbol(symbol) {
    const inputField = document.getElementById('calc-input');
    if (!inputField) return;
    
    const start = inputField.selectionStart;
    const end = inputField.selectionEnd;
    const currentText = inputField.value;

    const newText = currentText.substring(0, start) + symbol + currentText.substring(end);
    inputField.value = newText;

    const cursorPosition = start + symbol.length;
    inputField.setSelectionRange(cursorPosition, cursorPosition);
    
    inputField.focus();
    updatePreview();
}

function clearAll() {
    const inputField = document.getElementById('calc-input');
    const previewField = document.getElementById('live-preview');
    const debugInfo = document.getElementById('debug-info');
    
    if (inputField) inputField.value = '';
    if (previewField) previewField.textContent = '';
    if (debugInfo) debugInfo.textContent = '';
    if (inputField) inputField.focus();
}

function deleteLast() {
    const inputField = document.getElementById('calc-input');
    if (!inputField) return;
    
    const start = inputField.selectionStart;
    const currentText = inputField.value;
    if (start > 0) {
        inputField.value = currentText.substring(0, start - 1) + currentText.substring(start);
        inputField.setSelectionRange(start - 1, start - 1);
    }
    inputField.focus();
    updatePreview();
}

function updatePreview() {
    try {
        const inputField = document.getElementById('calc-input');
        const previewField = document.getElementById('live-preview');
        const debugInfo = document.getElementById('debug-info');
        
        if (!inputField || !previewField || !debugInfo) return;
        
        const expr = inputField.value;
        if (!expr.trim()) {
            previewField.textContent = '';
            debugInfo.textContent = '';
            return;
        }

        const { value, error, processed } = safeEvaluate(expr);
        
        debugInfo.textContent = '→ ' + processed;
        
        if (error) {
            previewField.textContent = error;
        } else if (value !== null) {
            const formatted = Number.isInteger(value) ? value : parseFloat(value.toFixed(10)).toString();
            previewField.textContent = '= ' + formatted;
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Erreur preview:', e);
        const previewField = document.getElementById('live-preview');
        if (previewField) previewField.textContent = "⚡ Erreur";
    }
}

function safeEvaluate(expression) {
    if (!expression.trim()) return { value: null, error: null, processed: expression };

    let processed = expression;

    // 1. Remplacer pi par Math.PI
    processed = processed.replace(/\bpi\b/g, 'Math.PI');

    // 2. Remplacer les fonctions simplifiées par Math.xxx
    processed = processed.replace(/\bsqrt\(/g, 'Math.sqrt(');
    processed = processed.replace(/\bln\(/g, 'Math.log(');
    processed = processed.replace(/\blog10\(/g, 'Math.log10(');
    processed = processed.replace(/\bsin\(/g, 'Math.sin(');
    processed = processed.replace(/\bcos\(/g, 'Math.cos(');
    processed = processed.replace(/\btan\(/g, 'Math.tan(');
    processed = processed.replace(/\bexp\(/g, 'Math.exp(');
    
    // 3. Gérer la puissance ^ -> **
    processed = processed.replace(/\^/g, '**');

    // 4. MULTIPLICATION IMPLICITE
    // Cas 1: nombre suivi de parenthèse: 2(3+4) → 2*(3+4)
    processed = processed.replace(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*\(/g, '$1*(');
    
    // Cas 2: parenthèse suivie de nombre: (2+3)4 → (2+3)*4
    processed = processed.replace(/\)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/g, ')*$1');
    
    // Cas 3: parenthèse suivie de parenthèse: (2+3)(4+5) → (2+3)*(4+5)
    processed = processed.replace(/\)\s*\(/g, ')*(');
    
    // Cas 4: nombre suivi de pi: 2pi → 2*Math.PI
    processed = processed.replace(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*Math\.PI/g, '$1*Math.PI');
    
    // Cas 5: pi suivi de nombre: pi2 → Math.PI*2
    processed = processed.replace(/Math\.PI\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/g, 'Math.PI*$1');
    
    // Cas 6: pi suivi de parenthèse: pi(2+3) → Math.PI*(2+3)
    processed = processed.replace(/Math\.PI\s*\(/g, 'Math.PI*(');
    
    // Cas 7: Gestion spéciale pour les expressions comme (6 (9) 4+6) (-2)
    processed = processed.replace(/(\d+)\s+(\d+)/g, '$1*$2');
    
    // Cas 8: Détection des nombres collés sans opérateur
    processed = processed.replace(/(\d+)\((\d+)\)(\d+)/g, '$1*$2*$3');
    processed = processed.replace(/(\d+)\((\d+)\)/g, '$1*$2');
    processed = processed.replace(/\((\d+)\)(\d+)/g, '($1)*$2');
    
    // Cas 9: Gestion des signes négatifs
    processed = processed.replace(/\)\s*-\s*(\d+)/g, ')-$1');

    try {
        // Vérifications basiques
        const openParen = (processed.match(/\(/g) || []).length;
        const closeParen = (processed.match(/\)/g) || []).length;
        if (openParen !== closeParen) {
            return { value: null, error: "❌ Parenthèses non équilibrées", processed };
        }

        // Vérification d'opérateurs consécutifs invalides (sauf ** qui est valide)
        const withoutPower = processed.replace(/\*\*/g, '@');
        if (/[\+\-\*\/]{2,}/.test(withoutPower)) {
            return { value: null, error: "❌ Opérateurs consécutifs", processed };
        }

        // Fonction plus sécurisée avec accès limité
        const fn = new Function('"use strict"; return (' + processed + ')');
        const result = fn();
        
        if (result === undefined || result === null || Number.isNaN(result)) {
            return { value: null, error: "❌ Résultat non défini", processed };
        }
        if (!isFinite(result)) {
            return { value: null, error: "❌ Division par zéro", processed };
        }
        
        return { value: result, error: null, processed };
    } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
        let errorMsg = "⚠️ Erreur syntaxe";
        if (err.message.includes("is not defined")) {
            errorMsg = "❌ Fonction inconnue";
        } else if (err.message.includes("Unexpected token")) {
            errorMsg = "❌ Expression invalide";
        }
        return { value: null, error: errorMsg, processed };
    }
}

function performExecution() {
    const inputField = document.getElementById('calc-input');
    const previewField = document.getElementById('live-preview');
    
    if (!inputField) return;
    
    const expression = inputField.value;
    if (!expression.trim()) return;

    const { value, error, processed } = safeEvaluate(expression);
    
    if (error) {
        if (previewField) previewField.textContent = error;
        return;
    }
    
    if (value !== null) {
        // Ajouter à l'historique
        saveToCalcHistory(expression, value, processed);
        
        // Ajouter aux calculs récents
        addRecentCalculation('calculatrice', `Calcul: ${expression}`, value);
        
        // Afficher le résultat
        inputField.value = value.toString();
        if (previewField) previewField.textContent = '';
        
        const debugInfo = document.getElementById('debug-info');
        if (debugInfo) debugInfo.textContent = '';
    }
}

function saveToCalcHistory(exp, res, processedExp) {
    const historyEntry = {
        id: Date.now(),
        expression: exp,
        result: res,
        processed: processedExp,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleString('fr-FR')
    };
    
    calcHistory.unshift(historyEntry);
    if (calcHistory.length > 20) calcHistory = calcHistory.slice(0, 20);
    
    localStorage.setItem(CALCULATOR_HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(calcHistory));
    updateCalcHistoryDisplay();
}

function updateCalcHistoryDisplay() {
    const container = document.getElementById('calc-history-list');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (calcHistory.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Aucun calcul récent</div>';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    calcHistory.forEach(entry => {
        const displayExp = entry.expression.length > 30 ? entry.expression.substring(0, 27) + '...' : entry.expression;
        
        html += `
            <div class="calculator-sci-history-item" onclick="useHistoryItem(${entry.id})">
                <span class="calculator-sci-history-exp">${displayExp}</span>
                <span class="calculator-sci-history-res">= ${formatNumberWithSettings(entry.result)}</span>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function useHistoryItem(id) {
    const entry = calcHistory.find(e => e.id === id);
    if (entry) {
        const inputField = document.getElementById('calc-input');
        if (inputField) {
            inputField.value = entry.result.toString();
            updatePreview();
        }
    }
}

function clearCalculatorHistory() {
    if (calcHistory.length === 0) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting('Effacer tout l\'historique des calculs ?')) {
        calcHistory = [];
        localStorage.setItem(CALCULATOR_HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(calcHistory));
        updateCalcHistoryDisplay();
        showNotification('Historique des calculs effacé', 'success');
    }
}

function saveCalculatorState() {
    const inputField = document.getElementById('calc-input');
    const stateName = prompt('Nom de l\'état de la calculatrice :', `Calcul_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    
    if (!stateName) return;
    
    const state = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: stateName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        expression: inputField ? inputField.value : ''
    };
    
    // On peut stocker dans une sauvegarde dédiée si nécessaire
    let calcStates = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('calculator_states') || '[]');
    calcStates.unshift(state);
    if (calcStates.length > 10) calcStates = calcStates.slice(0, 10);
    localStorage.setItem('calculator_states', JSON.stringify(calcStates));
    
    showNotification(`État "${stateName}" sauvegardé`, 'success');
}

// ============================================
// MODE H - DEVISE
// ============================================
const currencyFlags = {
    'EUR': 'eu', 'USD': 'us', 'GBP': 'gb', 'JPY': 'jp', 'CHF': 'ch',
    'CAD': 'ca', 'AUD': 'au', 'CNY': 'cn', 'INR': 'in', 'RUB': 'ru',
    'BRL': 'br', 'KRW': 'kr', 'MXN': 'mx', 'SGD': 'sg', 'HKD': 'hk',
    'NZD': 'nz', 'SEK': 'se', 'NOK': 'no', 'DKK': 'dk', 'PLN': 'pl',
    'TRY': 'tr', 'THB': 'th', 'IDR': 'id', 'MYR': 'my', 'PHP': 'ph',
    'VND': 'vn', 'AED': 'ae', 'SAR': 'sa', 'ZAR': 'za', 'EGP': 'eg'
};

async function loadExchangeRates() {
    showNotification('Chargement des taux de change...', 'info');
    
    try {
        const cached = localStorage.getItem(EXCHANGE_RATES_KEY);
        const cacheDate = localStorage.getItem('exchange_rates_date');
        
        if (cached && cacheDate) {
            const cacheTime = new Date(cacheDate);
            const now = new Date();
            const hoursDiff = (now - cacheTime) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
            
            if (hoursDiff < 24) {
                exchangeRates = JSON.parse(cached);
                updateCurrencySelects();
                convertCurrency();
                updateAllRates();
                showNotification('Taux de change chargés (cache)', 'success');
                return;
            }
        }
        
        const response = await fetch(`https://api.exchangerate-api.com/v4/latest/EUR`);
        
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`API error: ${response.status}`);
        
        const data = await response.json();
        exchangeRates = data.rates;
        exchangeRates['EUR'] = 1;
        
        localStorage.setItem(EXCHANGE_RATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(exchangeRates));
        localStorage.setItem('exchange_rates_date', new Date().toISOString());
        
        updateCurrencySelects();
        convertCurrency();
        updateAllRates();
        showNotification('Taux de change actualisés', 'success');
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erreur API taux de change:', error);
        exchangeRates = {
            'EUR': 1, 'USD': 1.08, 'GBP': 0.85, 'JPY': 160, 'CHF': 0.95,
            'CAD': 1.46, 'AUD': 1.64, 'CNY': 7.78, 'INR': 89.5,
            'BRL': 5.45, 'KRW': 1445, 'MXN': 18.2, 'SGD': 1.45,
            'HKD': 8.45, 'NZD': 1.78, 'SEK': 11.2, 'NOK': 11.5,
            'DKK': 7.45, 'PLN': 4.32, 'TRY': 34.8, 'THB': 38.9,
            'IDR': 17200, 'MYR': 5.12, 'PHP': 60.8, 'VND': 27000,
            'AED': 3.97, 'SAR': 4.05, 'ZAR': 20.1, 'EGP': 50.8
        };
        
        updateCurrencySelects();
        convertCurrency();
        updateAllRates();
        showNotification('Taux de change par défaut (API indisponible)', 'warning');
    }
}

function updateCurrencySelects() {
    const fromSelect = document.getElementById('fromCurrency');
    const toSelect = document.getElementById('toCurrency');
    
    if (!fromSelect || !toSelect) return;
    
    fromSelect.innerHTML = '';
    toSelect.innerHTML = '';
    
    const sortedCurrencies = Object.keys(exchangeRates).sort();
    
    sortedCurrencies.forEach(currency => {
        const option1 = document.createElement('option');
        option1.value = currency;
        option1.textContent = `${currency}`;
        fromSelect.appendChild(option1);
        
        const option2 = document.createElement('option');
        option2.value = currency;
        option2.textContent = `${currency}`;
        toSelect.appendChild(option2);
    });
    
    const lastFromCurrency = localStorage.getItem('lastFromCurrency') || 'EUR';
    const lastToCurrency = localStorage.getItem('lastToCurrency') || 'USD';
    
    if (sortedCurrencies.includes(lastFromCurrency)) fromSelect.value = lastFromCurrency;
    if (sortedCurrencies.includes(lastToCurrency)) toSelect.value = lastToCurrency;
    
    updateFlags();
}

function updateFlags() {
    const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency')?.value;
    const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency')?.value;
    
    const fromFlag = document.getElementById('fromFlag');
    const toFlag = document.getElementById('toFlag');
    
    if (fromCurrency && currencyFlags[fromCurrency] && fromFlag) {
        fromFlag.src = `https://flagcdn.com/w20/${currencyFlags[fromCurrency]}.png`;
        fromFlag.style.display = 'block';
    } else if (fromFlag) {
        fromFlag.src = '';
        fromFlag.style.display = 'none';
    }
    
    if (toCurrency && currencyFlags[toCurrency] && toFlag) {
        toFlag.src = `https://flagcdn.com/w20/${currencyFlags[toCurrency]}.png`;
        toFlag.style.display = 'block';
    } else if (toFlag) {
        toFlag.src = '';
        toFlag.style.display = 'none';
    }
    
    if (fromCurrency) localStorage.setItem('lastFromCurrency', fromCurrency);
    if (toCurrency) localStorage.setItem('lastToCurrency', toCurrency);
    
    convertCurrency();
}

function convertCurrency() {
    const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency')?.value;
    const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency')?.value;
    const fromAmount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('fromAmount')?.value) || 0;
    const toAmount = document.getElementById('toAmount');
    
    if (!fromCurrency || !toCurrency || !toAmount || !exchangeRates[fromCurrency] || !exchangeRates[toCurrency]) {
        if (toAmount) toAmount.value = '0.00';
        return;
    }
    
    try {
        const amountInEur = fromAmount / exchangeRates[fromCurrency];
        const convertedAmount = amountInEur * exchangeRates[toCurrency];
        
        toAmount.value = convertedAmount.toFixed(2);
        
        const rate = (exchangeRates[toCurrency] / exchangeRates[fromCurrency]).toFixed(4);
        const rateInfo = document.getElementById('rateInfo');
        if (rateInfo) {
            rateInfo.innerHTML = `Taux de change: 1 ${fromCurrency} = ${rate} ${toCurrency}`;
        }
        saveAllData();
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erreur conversion:', error);
        if (toAmount) toAmount.value = '0.00';
    }
}

function switchCurrencies() {
    const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency');
    const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency');
    const fromAmount = document.getElementById('fromAmount');
    const toAmount = document.getElementById('toAmount');
    
    if (!fromCurrency || !toCurrency || !fromAmount || !toAmount) return;
    
    const tempCurrency = fromCurrency.value;
    fromCurrency.value = toCurrency.value;
    toCurrency.value = tempCurrency;
    
    const tempAmount = fromAmount.value;
    fromAmount.value = toAmount.value;
    
    updateFlags();
    convertCurrency();
}

function updateAllRates() {
    const container = document.getElementById('allRatesGrid');
    if (!container) return;
    
    let html = '';
    const baseCurrency = 'EUR';
    
    Object.entries(exchangeRates)
        .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
        .forEach(([currency, rate]) => {
            if (currency === baseCurrency) return;
            
            const flagCode = currencyFlags[currency] || 'un';
            
            html += `
                <div style="padding: 12px; background: var(--bg-secondary); border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); transition: all 0.3s; cursor: pointer;" onclick="setCurrencyPair('${currency}')">
                    <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; margin-bottom: 8px;">
                        <img src="https://flagcdn.com/w20/${flagCode}.png" style="width: 24px; height: 18px; border-radius: 3px; object-fit: cover;">
                        <div style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--text-primary);">${currency}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size: 18px; color: var(--accent-color); margin-top: 5px;">${rate.toFixed(4)}</div>
                    <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">1 ${baseCurrency} = ${rate.toFixed(4)} ${currency}</div>
                </div>
            `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function setCurrencyPair(currency) {
    const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency');
    const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency');
    const fromAmount = document.getElementById('fromAmount');
    
    if (!fromCurrency || !toCurrency || !fromAmount) return;
    
    fromCurrency.value = 'EUR';
    toCurrency.value = currency;
    fromAmount.value = '100';
    updateFlags();
    convertCurrency();
    showNotification(`Paire EUR/${currency} sélectionnée`, 'info');
}

function saveConversionToHistory() {
    const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency')?.value;
    const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency')?.value;
    const fromAmount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('fromAmount')?.value) || 0;
    const toAmount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('toAmount')?.value) || 0;
    
    if (!fromCurrency || !toCurrency || fromAmount === 0) {
        showNotification('Entrez un montant à convertir', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const conversion = {
        id: Date.now(),
        date: new Date().toLocaleString('fr-FR'),
        from: { currency: fromCurrency, amount: fromAmount },
        to: { currency: toCurrency, amount: toAmount },
        rate: (exchangeRates[toCurrency] / exchangeRates[fromCurrency]).toFixed(4)
    };
    
    conversionHistory.unshift(conversion);
    if (conversionHistory.length > 20) conversionHistory = conversionHistory.slice(0, 20);
    
    localStorage.setItem('conversionHistory', JSON.stringify(conversionHistory));
    updateConversionHistoryDisplay();
    showNotification('Conversion sauvegardée', 'success');
    saveAllData();
}

function updateConversionHistoryDisplay() {
    const container = document.getElementById('conversionHistoryList');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (conversionHistory.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div style="padding: 30px; text-align: center; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <i class="fas fa-exchange-alt" style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 10px; opacity: 0.5;"></i>
                <p>Aucune conversion dans l'historique</p>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    
    conversionHistory.forEach(conv => {
        html += `
            <div class="history-item" style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr auto auto; gap: 10px; padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color); align-items: center; background: var(--bg-secondary);">
                <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${conv.date}</div>
                <div style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--text-primary);">${conv.from.amount.toFixed(2)} ${conv.from.currency}</div>
                <div style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-primary);">→</div>
                <div style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--success-color);">${conv.to.amount.toFixed(2)} ${conv.to.currency}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted); grid-column: 1 / -1;">Taux: ${conv.rate}</div>
