                <button onclick="deleteConversion(${conv.id})" style="padding: 5px 10px; background: var(--error-color); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 12px; grid-column: 4;">
                    <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                </button>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function deleteConversion(id) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette conversion de l\'historique ?')) {
        conversionHistory = conversionHistory.filter(conv => conv.id !== id);
        localStorage.setItem('conversionHistory', JSON.stringify(conversionHistory));
        updateConversionHistoryDisplay();
        showNotification('Conversion supprimée', 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function clearConversionHistory() {
    if (conversionHistory.length === 0) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting('Effacer tout l\'historique des conversions ?')) {
        conversionHistory = [];
        localStorage.setItem('conversionHistory', JSON.stringify([]));
        updateConversionHistoryDisplay();
        showNotification('Historique effacé', 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function exportHistoryToCSV() {
    if (conversionHistory.length === 0) {
        showNotification('Historique vide', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    let csv = 'Date;Montant Source;Devise Source;Montant Cible;Devise Cible;Taux de change\n';
    
    conversionHistory.forEach(conv => {
        csv += `${conv.date};${conv.from.amount};${conv.from.currency};${conv.to.amount};${conv.to.currency};${conv.rate}\n`;
    });
    
    const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    
    link.setAttribute('href', url);
    link.setAttribute('download', `historique_conversions_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`);
    link.style.visibility = 'hidden';
    
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    
    showNotification('Historique exporté en CSV', 'success');
}

// ============================================
// CALCULS RAPIDES
// ============================================
function updateCalculations() {
    const baseValue = parseFloat(document.getElementById('baseValue')?.value) || 0;
    const period = document.getElementById('timePeriod')?.value || 'day';
    const workingDays = parseInt(document.getElementById('workingDays')?.value) || 5;
    const hoursPerDay = parseFloat(document.getElementById('hoursPerDay')?.value) || 8;
    const holidays = parseInt(document.getElementById('holidaysPerYear')?.value) || 25;
    
    const workingDaysPerYear = (52 * workingDays) - holidays;
    const workingDaysPerMonth = (52 / 12) * workingDays;
    const workingDaysPerQuarter = workingDaysPerMonth * 3;
    const workingDaysPer5Years = workingDaysPerYear * 5;
    
    let conversions = {};
    
    switch(period) {
        case 'hour':
            conversions.hour = baseValue;
            conversions.day = baseValue * hoursPerDay;
            conversions.week = conversions.day * workingDays;
            conversions.month = conversions.week * (52 / 12);
            conversions.quarter = conversions.month * 3;
            conversions.year = conversions.day * workingDaysPerYear;
            conversions.fiveYears = conversions.year * 5;
            break;
        case 'day':
            conversions.hour = baseValue / hoursPerDay;
            conversions.day = baseValue;
            conversions.week = baseValue * workingDays;
            conversions.month = baseValue * workingDaysPerMonth;
            conversions.quarter = baseValue * workingDaysPerQuarter;
            conversions.year = baseValue * workingDaysPerYear;
            conversions.fiveYears = baseValue * workingDaysPer5Years;
            break;
        case 'week':
            conversions.hour = baseValue / (workingDays * hoursPerDay);
            conversions.day = baseValue / workingDays;
            conversions.week = baseValue;
            conversions.month = baseValue * (52 / 12);
            conversions.quarter = baseValue * 13;
            conversions.year = baseValue * 52;
            conversions.fiveYears = baseValue * 260;
            break;
        case 'month':
            conversions.hour = baseValue / (workingDaysPerMonth * hoursPerDay);
            conversions.day = baseValue / workingDaysPerMonth;
            conversions.week = baseValue / (workingDaysPerMonth / workingDays);
            conversions.month = baseValue;
            conversions.quarter = baseValue * 3;
            conversions.year = baseValue * 12;
            conversions.fiveYears = baseValue * 60;
            break;
        case 'quarter':
            conversions.hour = baseValue / (workingDaysPerQuarter * hoursPerDay);
            conversions.day = baseValue / workingDaysPerQuarter;
            conversions.week = baseValue / (workingDaysPerQuarter / workingDays);
            conversions.month = baseValue / 3;
            conversions.quarter = baseValue;
            conversions.year = baseValue * 4;
            conversions.fiveYears = baseValue * 20;
            break;
        case 'year':
            conversions.hour = baseValue / (workingDaysPerYear * hoursPerDay);
            conversions.day = baseValue / workingDaysPerYear;
            conversions.week = baseValue / (workingDaysPerYear / workingDays);
            conversions.month = baseValue / 12;
            conversions.quarter = baseValue / 4;
            conversions.year = baseValue;
            conversions.fiveYears = baseValue * 5;
            break;
        case 'fiveYears':
            conversions.hour = baseValue / (workingDaysPer5Years * hoursPerDay);
            conversions.day = baseValue / workingDaysPer5Years;
            conversions.week = baseValue / (workingDaysPer5Years / workingDays);
            conversions.month = baseValue / 60;
            conversions.quarter = baseValue / 20;
            conversions.year = baseValue / 5;
            conversions.fiveYears = baseValue;
            break;
    }
    
    const periodLabels = { 
        hour: 'par heure', day: 'par jour', week: 'par semaine', 
        month: 'par mois', quarter: 'par trimestre',
        year: 'par an', fiveYears: 'par 5 ans' 
    };
    
    const resultValue = document.getElementById('calcResultValue');
    if (resultValue) resultValue.textContent = `${formatNumber(baseValue)} € ${periodLabels[period]}`;
    
    const detailsContainer = document.getElementById('calcResultDetails');
    if (!detailsContainer) return;
    
    detailsContainer.innerHTML = '';
    
    const periods = [
        { key: 'hour', label: 'Par heure' }, { key: 'day', label: 'Par jour' },
        { key: 'week', label: 'Par semaine' }, { key: 'month', label: 'Par mois' },
        { key: 'quarter', label: 'Par trimestre' }, { key: 'year', label: 'Par an' },
        { key: 'fiveYears', label: 'Par 5 ans' }
    ];
    
    periods.forEach(p => {
        if (conversions[p.key] !== undefined) {
            const item = document.createElement('div');
            item.className = 'detail-item';
            item.innerHTML = `<span class="detail-label">${p.label}</span>
                             <span class="detail-value">${formatNumber(conversions[p.key])} €</span>`;
            detailsContainer.appendChild(item);
        }
    });
}

function resetCalculator() {
    const baseValue = document.getElementById('baseValue');
    const timePeriod = document.getElementById('timePeriod');
    const workingDays = document.getElementById('workingDays');
    const hoursPerDay = document.getElementById('hoursPerDay');
    const holidays = document.getElementById('holidaysPerYear');
    
    if (baseValue) baseValue.value = '1000';
    if (timePeriod) timePeriod.value = 'day';
    if (workingDays) workingDays.value = '5';
    if (hoursPerDay) hoursPerDay.value = '8';
    if (holidays) holidays.value = '25';
    
    updateCalculations();
    showNotification('Calculateur réinitialisé', 'success');
}

function saveCalculation() {
    const calculation = {
        id: Date.now(),
        date: new Date().toISOString(),
        baseValue: parseFloat(document.getElementById('baseValue')?.value) || 0,
        period: document.getElementById('timePeriod')?.value || 'day',
        workingDays: parseInt(document.getElementById('workingDays')?.value) || 5,
        hoursPerDay: parseFloat(document.getElementById('hoursPerDay')?.value) || 8,
        holidays: parseInt(document.getElementById('holidaysPerYear')?.value) || 25
    };
    
    let calculations = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CALC_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    calculations.unshift(calculation);
    if (calculations.length > 10) calculations = calculations.slice(0, 10);
    localStorage.setItem(CALC_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(calculations));
    
    showNotification('Calcul sauvegardé', 'success');
}

function formatNumber(value) {
    return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {
        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
        maximumFractionDigits: 2
    }).format(value);
}

// ============================================
// SAUVEGARDE AUTOMATIQUE STABILISÉE
// ============================================
function initAutoSave() {
    setInterval(() => {
        if (settings && settings.autoSave === true) {
            // Sauvegarder d'abord l'audio en cours si nécessaire
            if (pendingAudioBlob) {
                savePendingAudio();
            }
            saveAllData();
            saveNotes();
            saveTables();
            console.log('💾 Sauvegarde automatique (paramètre activé)');
        } else {
            console.log('⏸️ Sauvegarde automatique désactivée (paramètre)');
        }
    }, 30000);
    
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', function() {
        // Sauvegarde forcée de l'audio avant fermeture
        if (pendingAudioBlob) {
            savePendingAudio();
        }
        saveAllData();
        saveNotes();
        saveTables();
    });
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS DE SAUVEGARDE PAR MODE
// ============================================
function loadModeSaves() {
    modeASaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_A_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
    modeCSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_C_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
    modeBSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_B_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
    modeDSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_D_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
    modeGSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_G_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
    modeFSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_F_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
    modeTableSaves = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MODE_TABLE_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
    
    displayModeASaves();
    displayModeCSaves();
    displayModeBSaves();
    displayModeDSaves();
    displayModeGSaves();
    displayModeFSaves();
    displayTableSavesList();
}

// ============================================
// MODE A - SAUVEGARDE/RESTAURATION
// ============================================
function saveModeAState() {
    const stateName = prompt('Nom de l\'état :', `Comparer_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    if (!stateName) return;
    
    const blocks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#multiAContainer .ref-block')).map(block => ({
        ref: block.querySelector('.refA')?.value || '100',
        vals: block.querySelector('.valsA')?.value || '80 120 100'
    }));
    
    const state = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: stateName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        blocks: blocks
    };
    
    modeASaves.unshift(state);
    if (modeASaves.length > 10) modeASaves = modeASaves.slice(0, 10);
    
    localStorage.setItem(MODE_A_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeASaves));
    displayModeASaves();
    showNotification(`État "${stateName}" sauvegardé`, 'success');
}

function restoreModeAState(stateId) {
    const state = modeASaves.find(s => s.id === stateId);
    if (!state) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Restaurer l'état "${state.name}" ?\nLes données actuelles seront remplacées.`)) {
        const container = document.getElementById('multiAContainer');
        container.innerHTML = '';
        
        state.blocks.forEach(block => {
            const div = document.createElement("div");
            div.className = "ref-block";
            div.innerHTML = `
                <input type="number" class="refA" placeholder="Référence" value="${block.ref}" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculA()">
                <input type="text" class="valsA" placeholder="Valeurs (séparées par espaces)" value="${block.vals}" style="flex:1;" oninput="autoCalculA()">
                <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
            `;
            container.appendChild(div);
        });
        
        if (state.blocks.length === 0) addABlock();
        
        autoCalculA();
        showNotification(`État "${state.name}" restauré`, 'success');
    }
}

function deleteModeASave(stateId) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette sauvegarde ?')) {
        modeASaves = modeASaves.filter(s => s.id !== stateId);
        localStorage.setItem(MODE_A_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeASaves));
        displayModeASaves();
        showNotification('Sauvegarde supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

// ============================================
// MODE C - AVEC MISE À JOUR AUTOMATIQUE
// ============================================
function addCBlock() {
    const blockId = Date.now();
    const block = {
        id: blockId,
        name: `Bloc ${cBlockCounter}`,
        blockPercent: 0,
        variables: [
            { id: Date.now() + 1, name: `Variable 1`, value: 100, percent: 0 },
            { id: Date.now() + 2, name: `Variable 2`, value: 200, percent: 0 }
        ]
    };
    
    cBlocks.push(block);
    cBlockCounter++;
    renderCBlocks();
    updateCResults();
    saveAllData();
}

function renderCBlocks() {
    const container = document.getElementById('multiCContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (cBlocks.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<p style="text-align:center; color:var(--text-muted); padding: 20px;">Ajoutez un premier bloc</p>';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    
    cBlocks.forEach((block) => {
        html += `
            <div class="percentage-control" data-block-id="${block.id}">
                <div class="percentage-header">
                    <div class="percentage-title">
                        <i class="fas fa-percentage"></i>
                        <input type="text" value="${block.name}" oninput="updateCBlockName(${block.id}, this.value)" 
                               style="font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: bold; background: transparent; border: none; border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color); color: var(--text-primary); width: auto; min-width: 150px;">
                    </div>
                    <div class="percentage-badge">
                        % Bloc: ${block.blockPercent}%
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                <div style="margin: 20px 0; padding: 15px; background: var(--bg-secondary); border-radius: 8px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 20px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                        <div style="min-width: 120px;">
                            <strong>Pourcentage du bloc:</strong>
                        </div>
                        <div style="flex: 1; min-width: 200px;">
                            <input type="range" class="percentage-input" min="-100" max="100" value="${block.blockPercent}" 
                                   oninput="updateBlockPercent(${block.id}, this.value)" style="width: 100%;">
                        </div>
                        <div style="min-width: 100px;">
                            <input type="number" class="percentage-input" value="${block.blockPercent}" 
                                   oninput="updateBlockPercent(${block.id}, this.value)" style="width: 100px;" step="1" min="-100" max="100">
                            <span style="margin-left: 5px;">%</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="quick-presets">
                        <span style="color: var(--text-muted); margin-right: 10px;">Préréglages:</span>
                        <span class="preset-badge negative" onclick="applyPresetToBlock(${block.id}, -50)">-50%</span>
                        <span class="preset-badge negative" onclick="applyPresetToBlock(${block.id}, -25)">-25%</span>
                        <span class="preset-badge" onclick="applyPresetToBlock(${block.id}, 0)">0%</span>
                        <span class="preset-badge positive" onclick="applyPresetToBlock(${block.id}, 25)">+25%</span>
                        <span class="preset-badge positive" onclick="applyPresetToBlock(${block.id}, 50)">+50%</span>
                        <span class="preset-badge positive" onclick="applyPresetToBlock(${block.id}, 100)">+100%</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                ${block.variables.map(variable => `
                    <div class="percentage-item">
                        <div class="percentage-item-info">
                            <input type="text" value="${variable.name}" 
                                   oninput="updateVariableName(${block.id}, ${variable.id}, this.value)"
                                   style="background: transparent; border: none; border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color); color: var(--text-primary); width: 140px;">
                            <span style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 12px; margin-left: 5px;">(${variable.value.toFixed(2)})</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="percentage-value-container">
                            <input type="range" class="percentage-input" min="-100" max="100" value="${variable.percent}" 
                                   oninput="updateVariablePercent(${block.id}, ${variable.id}, this.value)" style="flex: 1;">
                            <div class="percentage-value-display ${variable.percent >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}">
                                ${variable.percent >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${variable.percent}%
                            </div>
                            <input type="number" class="percentage-input" value="${variable.percent}" 
                                   oninput="updateVariablePercent(${block.id}, ${variable.id}, this.value)" 
                                   style="width: 80px;" step="1" min="-100" max="100">
                        </div>
                        <div class="percentage-actions">
                            <button onclick="removeVariable(${block.id}, ${variable.id})" class="percentage-btn delete-btn" title="Supprimer">
                                <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `).join('')}
                
                <div style="margin-top: 20px; display: flex; gap: 10px; justify-content: flex-end;">
                    <button onclick="addVariableToBlock(${block.id})" class="secondary">
                        <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Ajouter une variable
                    </button>
                    <button onclick="removeCBlock(${block.id})" class="delete-btn">
                        <i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Supprimer ce bloc
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
    setTimeout(enhanceNumberInputs, 0);
}

function applyPresetToBlock(blockId, presetPercent) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) {
        block.blockPercent = presetPercent;
        block.variables.forEach(variable => {
            variable.percent = presetPercent;
        });
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        saveAllData();
        showNotification(`Pourcentage du bloc fixé à ${presetPercent}%`, 'success');
    }
}

function updateCBlockName(blockId, newName) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) block.name = newName;
    updateCResults();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateVariableName(blockId, variableId, newName) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) {
        const variable = block.variables.find(v => v.id === variableId);
        if (variable) variable.name = newName;
        updateCResults();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function updateBlockPercent(blockId, newPercent) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) {
        let percent = parseInt(newPercent) || 0;
        if (percent > 100) percent = 100;
        if (percent < -100) percent = -100;
        
        block.blockPercent = percent;
        block.variables.forEach(variable => {
            variable.percent = percent;
        });
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function updateVariablePercent(blockId, variableId, newPercent) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) {
        const variable = block.variables.find(v => v.id === variableId);
        if (variable) {
            let percent = parseInt(newPercent) || 0;
            if (percent > 100) percent = 100;
            if (percent < -100) percent = -100;
            
            variable.percent = percent;
            
            const allSamePercent = block.variables.every(v => v.percent === percent);
            if (allSamePercent) block.blockPercent = percent;
            
            renderCBlocks();
            updateCResults();
            saveAllData();
        }
    }
}

function addVariableToBlock(blockId) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) {
        block.variables.push({
            id: Date.now(),
            name: `Variable ${block.variables.length + 1}`,
            value: 100,
            percent: block.blockPercent
        });
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function removeVariable(blockId, variableId) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block && block.variables.length > 1) {
        block.variables = block.variables.filter(v => v.id !== variableId);
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        saveAllData();
    } else {
        showNotification('Un bloc doit avoir au moins une variable', 'warning');
    }
}

function removeCBlock(blockId) {
    if (cBlocks.length <= 1) {
        showNotification('Vous devez garder au moins un bloc', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    if (confirmWithSetting('Voulez-vous vraiment supprimer ce bloc ?')) {
        cBlocks = cBlocks.filter(b => b.id !== blockId);
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        showNotification('Bloc supprimé avec succès', 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function updateCResults() {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('cResultsBody');
    const tfoot = document.getElementById('cResultsTotal');
    if (!tbody || !tfoot) return;
    
    let html = '';
    
    let totalInitial = 0;
    let totalFinal = 0;
    let totalVariation = 0;
    
    cBlocks.forEach(block => {
        block.variables.forEach(variable => {
            const initial = variable.value;
            const final = initial * (1 + variable.percent / 100);
            const variation = final - initial;
            
            totalInitial += initial;
            totalFinal += final;
            totalVariation += variation;
            
            html += `<tr>
                <td>${block.name}</td>
                <td>${variable.name}</td>
                <td>${initial.toFixed(2)}</td>
                <td class="${variable.percent >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}">${variable.percent >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${variable.percent}%</td>
                <td>${final.toFixed(2)}</td>
                <td class="${variation >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}">${variation >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${variation.toFixed(2)}</td>
            </tr>`;
        });
    });
    
    tbody.innerHTML = html;
    
    tfoot.innerHTML = `
        <tr class="total-row">
            <td colspan="2"><strong>TOTAL GLOBAL</strong></td>
            <td><strong>${totalInitial.toFixed(2)}</strong></td>
            <td></td>
            <td><strong>${totalFinal.toFixed(2)}</strong></td>
            <td class="${totalVariation >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}">
                <strong>${totalVariation >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${totalVariation.toFixed(2)}</strong>
            </td>
        </tr>
    `;
    saveAllData();
}

function saveModeCState() {
    const stateName = prompt('Nom de l\'état :', `Pourcentage_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    if (!stateName) return;
    
    const state = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: stateName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        blocks: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cBlocks)),
        counter: cBlockCounter
    };
    
    modeCSaves.unshift(state);
    if (modeCSaves.length > 10) modeCSaves = modeCSaves.slice(0, 10);
    
    localStorage.setItem(MODE_C_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeCSaves));
    displayModeCSaves();
    showNotification(`État "${stateName}" sauvegardé`, 'success');
}

function restoreModeCState(stateId) {
    const state = modeCSaves.find(s => s.id === stateId);
    if (!state) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Restaurer l'état "${state.name}" ?\nLes données actuelles seront remplacées.`)) {
        cBlocks = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.blocks));
        cBlockCounter = state.counter;
        
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        showNotification(`État "${state.name}" restauré`, 'success');
    }
}

function deleteModeCSave(stateId) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette sauvegarde ?')) {
        modeCSaves = modeCSaves.filter(s => s.id !== stateId);
        localStorage.setItem(MODE_C_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeCSaves));
        displayModeCSaves();
        showNotification('Sauvegarde supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

// ============================================
// MODE B - SAUVEGARDE/RESTAURATION
// ============================================
function saveModeBState() {
    const stateName = prompt('Nom de l\'état :', `Budget_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    if (!stateName) return;
    
    const state = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: stateName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        categories: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(budgetCategories)),
        catCount: budgetCatCount,
        budget: document.getElementById('budget')?.value || '2000'
    };
    
    modeBSaves.unshift(state);
    if (modeBSaves.length > 10) modeBSaves = modeBSaves.slice(0, 10);
    
    localStorage.setItem(MODE_B_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeBSaves));
    displayModeBSaves();
    showNotification(`État "${stateName}" sauvegardé`, 'success');
}

function restoreModeBState(stateId) {
    const state = modeBSaves.find(s => s.id === stateId);
    if (!state) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Restaurer l'état "${state.name}" ?\nLes données actuelles seront remplacées.`)) {
        budgetCategories = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.categories));
        budgetCatCount = state.catCount;
        const budgetInput = document.getElementById('budget');
        if (budgetInput) budgetInput.value = state.budget;
        
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        showNotification(`État "${state.name}" restauré`, 'success');
    }
}

function deleteModeBSave(stateId) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette sauvegarde ?')) {
        modeBSaves = modeBSaves.filter(s => s.id !== stateId);
        localStorage.setItem(MODE_B_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeBSaves));
        displayModeBSaves();
        showNotification('Sauvegarde supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

// ============================================
// MODE BUDGET - AVEC MISE À JOUR AUTOMATIQUE
// ============================================
function addBudgetCategory() {
    const category = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: `Catégorie ${budgetCatCount++}`,
        percent: 0,
        subs: []
    };
    
    budgetCategories.push(category);
    renderBudgetCategories();
    updateBudgetRecap();
    saveAllData();
}

function removeBudgetCategory(index) {
    if (budgetCategories.length <= 1) {
        showNotification('Vous devez garder au moins une catégorie', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette catégorie ?')) {
        budgetCategories.splice(index, 1);
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function addBudgetSubcategory(catIndex) {
    const category = budgetCategories[catIndex];
    if (category) {
        category.subs.push({
            id: Date.now(),
            name: `Sous-catégorie ${category.subs.length + 1}`,
            percent: 0
        });
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function removeBudgetSubcategory(catIndex, subIndex) {
    const category = budgetCategories[catIndex];
    if (category && category.subs.length > 0) {
        category.subs.splice(subIndex, 1);
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function renameBudgetCategory(index) {
    const newName = prompt("Renommer la catégorie", budgetCategories[index].name);
    if (newName && newName.trim() !== '') {
        budgetCategories[index].name = newName.trim();
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function renameBudgetSubcategory(catIndex, subIndex) {
    const sub = budgetCategories[catIndex].subs[subIndex];
    if (sub) {
        const newName = prompt("Renommer la sous-catégorie", sub.name);
        if (newName && newName.trim() !== '') {
            sub.name = newName.trim();
            renderBudgetCategories();
            updateBudgetRecap();
            saveAllData();
        }
    }
}

function adjustCategoryPercent(index, value) {
    const category = budgetCategories[index];
    if (category) {
        value = parseFloat(value) || 0;
        
        const otherPercent = budgetCategories.reduce((sum, cat, i) =>
            i !== index ? sum + cat.percent : sum, 0);
        
        const maxAllowed = 100 - otherPercent;
        category.percent = Math.min(Math.max(value, 0), maxAllowed);
        
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function adjustSubcategoryPercent(catIndex, subIndex, value) {
    const category = budgetCategories[catIndex];
    const subcategory = category.subs[subIndex];
    
    if (subcategory) {
        value = parseFloat(value) || 0;
        
        const otherPercent = category.subs.reduce((sum, sub, i) =>
            i !== subIndex ? sum + sub.percent : sum, 0);
        
        const maxAllowed = 100 - otherPercent;
        subcategory.percent = Math.min(Math.max(value, 0), maxAllowed);
        
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function renderBudgetCategories() {
    const container = document.getElementById('budgetCategories');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (budgetCategories.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px 20px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <i class="fas fa-wallet fa-2x" style="margin-bottom: 15px;"></i>
                <p>Ajoutez une première catégorie pour commencer</p>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    
    budgetCategories.forEach((cat, catIndex) => {
        html += `
            <div class="category">
                <div class="category-content">
                    <span class="editable" ondblclick="renameBudgetCategory(${catIndex})">
                        <strong>${cat.name}</strong>
                    </span>
                    <input type="number" class="budget-input" value="${cat.percent}" min="0" max="100" step="0.01" 
                        oninput="adjustCategoryPercent(${catIndex}, this.value)">
                    <span class="percent-label">%</span>
                    <button class="delete-btn" onclick="removeBudgetCategory(${catIndex})">
                        <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
        `;
        
        cat.subs.forEach((sub, subIndex) => {
            html += `
                <div class="subcategory">
                    <div class="subcategory-content">
                        <span class="editable" ondblclick="renameBudgetSubcategory(${catIndex}, ${subIndex})">
                            ${sub.name}
                        </span>
                        <input type="number" class="budget-input" value="${sub.percent}" min="0" max="100" step="0.01"
                            oninput="adjustSubcategoryPercent(${catIndex}, ${subIndex}, this.value)">
                        <span class="percent-label">%</span>
                        <button class="delete-btn" onclick="removeBudgetSubcategory(${catIndex}, ${subIndex})">
                            <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        });
        
        html += `
                <div style="text-align: right; margin-top: 10px;">
                    <button onclick="addBudgetSubcategory(${catIndex})" class="secondary">
                        <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Sous-catégorie
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
    setTimeout(enhanceNumberInputs, 0);
}

function updateBudgetRecap() {
    const budget = parseFloat(document.getElementById('budget')?.value) || 0;
    const tbody = document.getElementById('budgetRecap');
    if (!tbody) return;
    
    let html = '';
    let totalPercent = 0;
    
    budgetCategories.forEach(cat => {
        const catAmount = budget * cat.percent / 100;
        totalPercent += cat.percent;
        
        if (cat.subs.length === 0) {
            html += `
                <tr class="category-row">
                    <td>${cat.name}</td>
                    <td>-</td>
                    <td>${cat.percent}%</td>
                    <td>${catAmount.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                </tr>
            `;
        } else {
            html += `
                <tr class="category-row">
                    <td>${cat.name}</td>
                    <td>-</td>
                    <td>${cat.percent}%</td>
                    <td>${catAmount.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                </tr>
            `;
            
            cat.subs.forEach(sub => {
                const globalPercent = cat.percent * sub.percent / 100;
                const amount = budget * globalPercent / 100;
                
                html += `
                    <tr class="sub-row">
                        <td></td>
                        <td>${sub.name}</td>
                        <td>${globalPercent.toFixed(2)}%</td>
                        <td>${amount.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            });
        }
    });
    
    html += `
        <tr class="total-row">
            <td>TOTAL</td>
            <td>-</td>
            <td>${totalPercent}%</td>
            <td>${budget.toFixed(2)} €</td>
        </tr>
    `;
    
    tbody.innerHTML = html;
    saveAllData();
}

// ============================================
// MODE D - AVEC MISE À JOUR AUTOMATIQUE
// ============================================
function saveModeDState() {
    const stateName = prompt('Nom de l\'état :', `Évolution_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    if (!stateName) return;
    
    const state = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: stateName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        dates: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(dDates)),
        series: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(dSeries)),
        useDates: document.getElementById('useDates')?.checked || true
    };
    
    modeDSaves.unshift(state);
    if (modeDSaves.length > 10) modeDSaves = modeDSaves.slice(0, 10);
    
    localStorage.setItem(MODE_D_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeDSaves));
    displayModeDSaves();
    showNotification(`État "${stateName}" sauvegardé`, 'success');
}

function restoreModeDState(stateId) {
    const state = modeDSaves.find(s => s.id === stateId);
    if (!state) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Restaurer l'état "${state.name}" ?\nLes données actuelles seront remplacées.`)) {
        dDates = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.dates));
        dSeries = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.series));
        const useDates = document.getElementById('useDates');
        if (useDates) useDates.checked = state.useDates;
        
        updateDTable();
        calculerD();
        showNotification(`État "${state.name}" restauré`, 'success');
    }
}

function deleteModeDSave(stateId) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette sauvegarde ?')) {
        modeDSaves = modeDSaves.filter(s => s.id !== stateId);
        localStorage.setItem(MODE_D_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeDSaves));
        displayModeDSaves();
        showNotification('Sauvegarde supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

// ============================================
// MODE D - FONCTIONS AVEC MISE À JOUR AUTOMATIQUE
// ============================================
function toggleDates() {
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function addDDate() {
    const useDatesCheckbox = document.getElementById('useDates');
    const useDates = useDatesCheckbox ? useDatesCheckbox.checked : true;
    const id = Date.now();
    
    if (useDates) {
        const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        dDates.push({ id, date });
    } else {
        dDates.push({ id, label: `Point ${dDates.length + 1}` });
    }
    
    dSeries.forEach(s => s.values[id] = 0);
    
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function removeDDate(id) {
    dDates = dDates.filter(d => d.id !== id);
    dSeries.forEach(s => delete s.values[id]);
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function addDSeries() {
    const newSeries = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: `Série ${dSeries.length + 1}`,
        values: {},
        color: '#' + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16)
    };
    dDates.forEach(d => newSeries.values[d.id] = 0);
    dSeries.push(newSeries);
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function removeDSeries(id) {
    dSeries = dSeries.filter(s => s.id !== id);
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDDate(id, newDate) {
    const dateObj = dDates.find(d => d.id === id);
    if (dateObj) dateObj.date = newDate;
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDPointLabel(id, newLabel) {
    const pointObj = dDates.find(d => d.id === id);
    if (pointObj) pointObj.label = newLabel;
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDSeriesName(id, newName) {
    const series = dSeries.find(s => s.id === id);
    if (series) series.name = newName;
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDSeriesColor(id, newColor) {
    const series = dSeries.find(s => s.id === id);
    if (series) series.color = newColor;
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDSeriesValue(seriesId, dateId, newValue) {
    const series = dSeries.find(s => s.id === seriesId);
    if (series) series.values[dateId] = parseFloat(newValue) || 0;
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDTable() {
    const dateHeader = document.getElementById('dateHeaderRow');
    const seriesBody = document.getElementById('seriesBody');
    if (!dateHeader || !seriesBody) return;
    
    const useDatesCheckbox = document.getElementById('useDates');
    const useDates = useDatesCheckbox ? useDatesCheckbox.checked : true;
    
    let headerHTML = '<th style="min-width: 160px;">Série / Date</th>';
    dDates.forEach(d => {
        if (useDates) {
            headerHTML += `
                <th>
                    <input type="date" value="${d.date || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}" oninput="updateDDate(${d.id}, this.value)" style="width:120px;">
                    <button onclick="removeDDate(${d.id})" style="padding:2px 6px;font-size:11px; background: var(--error-color); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 3px; cursor: pointer;">✖</button>
                </th>
            `;
        } else {
            headerHTML += `
                <th>
                    <input type="text" value="${d.label || `Point ${dDates.indexOf(d) + 1}`}" oninput="updateDPointLabel(${d.id}, this.value)" style="width:100px;">
                    <button onclick="removeDDate(${d.id})" style="padding:2px 6px;font-size:11px; background: var(--error-color); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 3px; cursor: pointer;">✖</button>
                </th>
            `;
        }
    });
    dateHeader.innerHTML = headerHTML;
    
    seriesBody.innerHTML = '';
    dSeries.forEach(s => {
        let row = document.createElement('tr');
        let rowHTML = `
            <td>
                <input type="text" value="${s.name}" oninput="updateDSeriesName(${s.id}, this.value)" style="width:120px;">
                <input type="color" value="${s.color}" oninput="updateDSeriesColor(${s.id}, this.value)" title="Couleur" style="width: 30px; height: 30px;">
                <button onclick="removeDSeries(${s.id})" style="padding:2px 6px;font-size:11px; background: var(--error-color); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 3px; cursor: pointer;">✖</button>
            </td>
        `;
        
        dDates.forEach(d => {
            const val = s.values[d.id] || 0;
            rowHTML += `
                <td>
                    <input type="number" value="${val}" 
                           oninput="updateDSeriesValue(${s.id}, ${d.id}, this.value)" 
                           style="width:80px;" step="0.01">
                </td>
            `;
        });
        
        row.innerHTML = rowHTML;
        seriesBody.appendChild(row);
    });
    
    setTimeout(enhanceNumberInputs, 0);
}

function sortDDates() {
    const useDatesCheckbox = document.getElementById('useDates');
    const useDates = useDatesCheckbox ? useDatesCheckbox.checked : true;
    
    if (useDates) {
        dDates.sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date) - new Date(b.date));
    }
    updateDTable();
    calculerD();
    saveAllData();
}

function calculerD() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('graphD');
    if (!canvas) return;
    
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    
    if (chartD) chartD.destroy();
    
    const useDatesCheckbox = document.getElementById('useDates');
    const useDates = useDatesCheckbox ? useDatesCheckbox.checked : true;
    let labels;
    
    if (useDates) {
        labels = dDates.map(d => d.date);
    } else {
        labels = dDates.map(d => d.label || `Point ${dDates.indexOf(d) + 1}`);
    }
    
    const datasets = dSeries.map(s => ({
        label: s.name,
        data: dDates.map(d => s.values[d.id] || 0),
        borderColor: s.color,
        backgroundColor: s.color + '20',
        fill: false,
        tension: 0.1,
        pointRadius: 5,
        pointHoverRadius: 8
    }));
    
    chartD = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'line',
        data: { labels, datasets },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            plugins: {
                legend: { position: 'top' },
                title: { display: true, text: 'Évolution temporelle' }
            },
            scales: {
                y: {
                    beginAtZero: true,
                    grid: { color: 'var(--border-light)' },
                    ticks: { color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }
                },
                x: {
                    grid: { color: 'var(--border-light)' },
                    ticks: { color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }
                }
            }
        }
    });
    saveAllData();
}

// ============================================
// MODE F - AVEC MISE À JOUR AUTOMATIQUE
// ============================================
function saveModeFState() {
    const stateName = prompt('Nom de l\'état :', `Circulaire_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    if (!stateName) return;
    
    const blocks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block')).map(block => ({
        label: block.querySelector('.labelF')?.value || '',
        value: block.querySelector('.valF')?.value || '0',
        color: block.querySelector('.colorF')?.value || '#3498db',
        originalValue: block.getAttribute('data-original-value'),
        isSelected: block.querySelector('.fusion-toggle-btn')?.getAttribute('data-selected') === 'true',
        isHidden: block.classList.contains('hidden-legend')
    }));
    
    const state = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: stateName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        blocks: blocks
    };
    
    modeFSaves.unshift(state);
    if (modeFSaves.length > 10) modeFSaves = modeFSaves.slice(0, 10);
    
    localStorage.setItem(MODE_F_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeFSaves));
    displayModeFSaves();
    showNotification(`État "${stateName}" sauvegardé`, 'success');
}

function restoreModeFState(stateId) {
    const state = modeFSaves.find(s => s.id === stateId);
    if (!state) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Restaurer l'état "${state.name}" ?\nLes données actuelles seront remplacées.`)) {
        const container = document.getElementById("multiFContainer");
        if (!container) return;
        
        container.innerHTML = '';
        
        state.blocks.forEach(blockData => {
            const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
            const div = document.createElement("div");
            div.className = "ref-block";
            div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
            
            if (blockData.originalValue) div.setAttribute('data-original-value', blockData.originalValue);
            if (blockData.isHidden) div.classList.add('hidden-legend');
            
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                    <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="${blockData.label}" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="${blockData.value}" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <input type="color" class="colorF" value="${blockData.color}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="${blockData.isSelected}" style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none; background: ${blockData.isSelected ? 'var(--success-color)' : 'var(--bg-tertiary)'}; color: ${blockData.isSelected ? 'white' : 'var(--text-primary)'};">${blockData.isSelected ? '✓' : '✗'}</div>
                    <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
                </div>
            `;
            
            container.appendChild(div);
        });
        
        if (state.blocks.length === 0) {
            addFBlock();
            addFBlock();
        }
        
        updateSelectedCount();
        updateVisibleCount();
        recalculatePercentagesF();
        calculerF();
        showNotification(`État "${state.name}" restauré`, 'success');
    }
}

function deleteModeFSave(stateId) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette sauvegarde ?')) {
        modeFSaves = modeFSaves.filter(s => s.id !== stateId);
        localStorage.setItem(MODE_F_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeFSaves));
        displayModeFSaves();
        showNotification('Sauvegarde supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

// ============================================
// MODE F - FONCTIONS AVEC MISE À JOUR AUTOMATIQUE
// ============================================
function addFBlock() {
    const container = document.getElementById("multiFContainer");
    if (!container) return;
    
    const id = Date.now();
    
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = "ref-block";
    div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
    div.setAttribute('data-original-value', '100');
    div.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
            <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="Nouvelle catégorie" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="100" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <input type="color" class="colorF" value="#${Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16)}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="false" data-original-value="100" style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none;">✗</div>
            <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
        </div>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(div);
    autoCalculF();
    updateSelectedCount();
    updateVisibleCount();
    calculerF();
    saveAllData();
    
    setTimeout(enhanceNumberInputs, 0);
}

function removeF(btn) {
    const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
    if (block) {
        block.remove();
        autoCalculF();
        updateSelectedCount();
        updateVisibleCount();
        calculerF();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function autoCalculF() {
    recalculatePercentagesF();
    calculerF();
    saveAllData();
}

function calculerF() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('graphF');
    if (!canvas) return;
    
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    
    if (chartF) chartF.destroy();
    
    historiqueF = [];
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    
    const visibleBlocks = Array.from(blocks).filter(block => !block.classList.contains('hidden-legend'));
    
    visibleBlocks.forEach(block => {
        historiqueF.push({
            label: block.querySelector('.labelF')?.value || "Sans nom",
            val: parseFloat(block.querySelector('.valF')?.value) || 0,
            color: block.querySelector('.colorF')?.value || '#3498db',
            type: block.getAttribute('data-original-value') ? 'original' : 'merged'
        });
    });
    
    const tableauTbody = document.querySelector('#tableauF tbody');
    const tableauTfoot = document.getElementById('tableauFTotal');
    
    if (historiqueF.length === 0) {
        if (tableauTbody) tableauTbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-muted); padding: 20px;">Aucune catégorie visible</td></tr>';
        if (tableauTfoot) tableauTfoot.innerHTML = '';
        return;
    }
    
    const total = historiqueF.reduce((sum, cat) => sum + cat.val, 0);
    
    const data = {
        labels: historiqueF.map(cat => cat.label),
        datasets: [{
            data: historiqueF.map(cat => cat.val),
            backgroundColor: historiqueF.map(cat => cat.color),
            borderWidth: 2,
            borderColor: 'var(--bg-secondary)'
        }]
    };
    
    chartF = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'pie',
        data: data,
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            plugins: {
                legend: { 
                    position: 'right',
                    onClick: function(evt, legendItem, legend) {
                        evt.stopPropagation();
                        
                        const index = legendItem.index;
                        const label = this.chart.data.labels[index];
                        
                        const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
                        blocks.forEach(block => {
                            if (block.querySelector('.labelF')?.value === label) {
                                block.classList.toggle('hidden-legend');
                            }
                        });
                        
                        recalculatePercentagesF();
                        calculerF();
                    }
                },
                title: { display: true, text: `Répartition - Total: ${total.toFixed(2)}` },
                datalabels: {
                    formatter: (value, context) => {
                        if (total === 0) return '0%';
                        const percentage = (value / total * 100).toFixed(1);
                        return context.chart.data.labels[context.dataIndex] + '\n' + percentage + '%';
                    },
                    color: '#fff',
                    font: { weight: 'bold', size: 12 },
                    textAlign: 'center'
                }
            }
        },
        plugins: [ChartDataLabels]
    });
    
    updateFTable(total);
    saveAllData();
}

function recalculatePercentagesF() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    
    const visibleBlocks = Array.from(blocks).filter(block => !block.classList.contains('hidden-legend'));
    
    const totalVisible = visibleBlocks.reduce((sum, block) => {
        const value = parseFloat(block.querySelector('.valF')?.value) || 0;
        return sum + value;
    }, 0);
    
    updateFTable(totalVisible);
    updateVisibleCount();
}

function updateFTable(totalVisible = null) {
    const tbody = document.querySelector('#tableauF tbody');
    const tfoot = document.getElementById('tableauFTotal');
    
    if (!tbody || !tfoot) return;
    
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    
    let total;
    if (totalVisible !== null) {
        total = totalVisible;
    } else {
        total = Array.from(blocks).reduce((sum, block) => {
            const value = parseFloat(block.querySelector('.valF')?.value) || 0;
            return sum + value;
        }, 0);
    }
    
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    
    blocks.forEach(block => {
        const label = block.querySelector('.labelF')?.value || 'Sans nom';
        const value = parseFloat(block.querySelector('.valF')?.value) || 0;
        const color = block.querySelector('.colorF')?.value || '#3498db';
        const type = block.getAttribute('data-original-value') ? 'original' : 'merged';
        const isHidden = block.classList.contains('hidden-legend');
        
        const percentage = (total === 0 || isHidden) ? 0 : (value / total * 100);
        
        const typeBadge = type === 'merged' ?
            '<span style="background: #9b59b6; color: white; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 11px;">Fusionnée</span>' :
            '<span style="background: var(--success-color); color: white; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 11px;">Originale</span>';
        
        const visibilityBadge = isHidden ? 
            '<span style="background: var(--warning-color); color: white; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 11px; margin-left: 5px;">Cachée</span>' :
            '<span style="background: var(--success-color); color: white; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 11px; margin-left: 5px;">Visible</span>';
        
        tbody.innerHTML += `<tr style="${isHidden ? 'opacity: 0.5; background: var(--bg-tertiary);' : ''}">
            <td>
                <span style="display:inline-block;width:12px;height:12px;background:${color};margin-right:8px;border-radius:2px;"></span>
                ${label}
            </td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">${value.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">
                ${isHidden ? '-' : `${percentage.toFixed(1)}%`}
            </td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">${typeBadge}</td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">${visibilityBadge}</td>
        </tr>`;
    });
    
    tfoot.innerHTML = `
        <tr style="font-weight: bold; background: rgba(52, 152, 219, 0.1);">
            <td style="text-align: center;">TOTAL (${totalVisible !== null ? 'visibles' : 'tous'})</td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">${total.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">100%</td>
            <td></td>
            <td></td>
        </tr>
    `;
}

function toggleFusionSelection(btn) {
    if (!btn) return;
    const isSelected = btn.getAttribute('data-selected') === 'true';
    if (isSelected) {
        btn.textContent = '✗';
        btn.style.background = 'var(--bg-tertiary)';
        btn.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
        btn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'false');
    } else {
        btn.textContent = '✓';
        btn.style.background = 'var(--success-color)';
        btn.style.color = 'white';
        btn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'true');
    }
    updateSelectedCount();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateSelectedCount() {
    const selected = document.querySelectorAll('.fusion-toggle-btn[data-selected="true"]');
    const selectedCount = document.getElementById('selectedCount');
    if (selectedCount) selectedCount.textContent = selected.length;
}

function updateVisibleCount() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    const visible = Array.from(blocks).filter(block => !block.classList.contains('hidden-legend')).length;
    
    const visibleCountElement = document.getElementById('visibleCount');
    if (visibleCountElement) visibleCountElement.textContent = visible;
}

function selectAllForFusion(select) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.fusion-toggle-btn').forEach(btn => {
        if (select) {
            btn.textContent = '✓';
            btn.style.background = 'var(--success-color)';
            btn.style.color = 'white';
            btn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'true');
        } else {
            btn.textContent = '✗';
            btn.style.background = 'var(--bg-tertiary)';
            btn.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
            btn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'false');
        }
    });
    updateSelectedCount();
    saveAllData();
}

function mergeSelectedCategories() {
    const selected = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.fusion-toggle-btn[data-selected="true"]'));
    if (selected.length < 2) {
        showNotification('Sélectionnez au moins 2 catégories à fusionner.', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const fusionNameInput = document.getElementById('fusionName');
    const fusionName = fusionNameInput ? fusionNameInput.value || 'Fusionnée' : 'Fusionnée';
    let totalVal = 0;
    let color = '#3498db';
    
    const originalCategories = [];
    
    selected.forEach((btn, index) => {
        const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
        if (!block) return;
        
        const label = block.querySelector('.labelF')?.value || '';
        const val = parseFloat(block.querySelector('.valF')?.value) || 0;
        totalVal += val;
        
        originalCategories.push({
            label: label,
            value: val,
            color: block.querySelector('.colorF')?.value || '#3498db',
            originalValue: block.getAttribute('data-original-value'),
            isHidden: block.classList.contains('hidden-legend')
        });
        
        if (index === 0) color = block.querySelector('.colorF')?.value || '#3498db';
    });
    
    selected.forEach(btn => {
        const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
        if (block) block.remove();
    });
    
    const container = document.getElementById("multiFContainer");
    if (!container) return;
    
    const id = Date.now();
    
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = "ref-block";
    div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
    div.setAttribute('data-original-categories', JSON.stringify(originalCategories));
    div.setAttribute('data-is-merged', 'true');
    
    div.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
            <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="${fusionName}" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="${totalVal}" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <input type="color" class="colorF" value="${color}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="false" data-original-categories='${JSON.stringify(originalCategories)}' style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none;">✗</div>
            <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top: 10px; font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">
            <i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> Fusion de ${selected.length} catégories
        </div>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(div);
    
    if (fusionNameInput) fusionNameInput.value = '';
    selectAllForFusion(false);
    recalculatePercentagesF();
    calculerF();
    showNotification(`${selected.length} catégories fusionnées en "${fusionName}"`, 'success');
    saveAllData();
}

function splitSelectedCategory() {
    const selected = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.fusion-toggle-btn[data-selected="true"]'));
    if (selected.length !== 1) {
        showNotification('Sélectionnez une seule catégorie fusionnée à séparer.', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const btn = selected[0];
    const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
    if (!block) return;
    
    const originalCategoriesJSON = block.getAttribute('data-original-categories');
    
    if (!originalCategoriesJSON) {
        showNotification('Cette catégorie n\'est pas fusionnée ou ne peut pas être séparée.', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const originalCategories = JSON.parse(originalCategoriesJSON);
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Séparer cette catégorie en ${originalCategories.length} catégories originales ?`)) {
        block.remove();
        
        const container = document.getElementById("multiFContainer");
        if (!container) return;
        
        originalCategories.forEach(cat => {
            const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
            const div = document.createElement("div");
            div.className = "ref-block";
            div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
            div.setAttribute('data-original-value', cat.originalValue || cat.value);
            
            if (cat.isHidden) div.classList.add('hidden-legend');
            
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                    <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="${cat.label}" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="${cat.value}" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <input type="color" class="colorF" value="${cat.color}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="false" data-original-value="${cat.originalValue || cat.value}" style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none;">✗</div>
                    <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
                </div>
            `;
            
            container.appendChild(div);
        });
        
        selectAllForFusion(false);
        recalculatePercentagesF();
        calculerF();
        showNotification(`Catégorie séparée en ${originalCategories.length} catégories originales`, 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function resetToOriginalValues() {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Réinitialiser toutes les catégories à leurs valeurs originales ?')) {
        const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
        
        blocks.forEach(block => {
            const originalValue = block.getAttribute('data-original-value');
            if (originalValue) {
                const valInput = block.querySelector('.valF');
                if (valInput) valInput.value = originalValue;
            }
            
            const toggleBtn = block.querySelector('.fusion-toggle-btn');
            if (toggleBtn) {
                toggleBtn.textContent = '✗';
                toggleBtn.style.background = 'var(--bg-tertiary)';
                toggleBtn.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
                toggleBtn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'false');
            }
        });
        
        updateSelectedCount();
        recalculatePercentagesF();
        calculerF();
        showNotification('Valeurs réinitialisées', 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function showAllCategories() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    blocks.forEach(block => block.classList.remove('hidden-legend'));
    recalculatePercentagesF();
    calculerF();
    showNotification('Toutes les catégories sont maintenant visibles', 'success');
}

function hideAllCategories() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    blocks.forEach(block => block.classList.add('hidden-legend'));
    recalculatePercentagesF();
    calculerF();
    showNotification('Toutes les catégories sont maintenant masquées', 'warning');
}

function toggleSelectedCategoriesVisibility() {
    const selectedButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.fusion-toggle-btn[data-selected="true"]');
    if (selectedButtons.length === 0) {
        showNotification('Sélectionnez d\'abord des catégories', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    selectedButtons.forEach(btn => {
        const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
        if (block) block.classList.toggle('hidden-legend');
    });
    recalculatePercentagesF();
    calculerF();
    showNotification('Visibilité des catégories sélectionnées inversée', 'info');
}

// ============================================
// MODE G - AVEC MISE À JOUR AUTOMATIQUE
// ============================================
function saveModeGState() {
    const stateName = prompt('Nom de l\'état :', `TVA_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    if (!stateName) return;
    
    const blocks = collectModeGData();
    
    const state = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: stateName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        blocks: blocks
    };
    
    modeGSaves.unshift(state);
    if (modeGSaves.length > 10) modeGSaves = modeGSaves.slice(0, 10);
    
    localStorage.setItem(MODE_G_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeGSaves));
    displayModeGSaves();
    showNotification(`État "${stateName}" sauvegardé`, 'success');
}

function restoreModeGState(stateId) {
    const state = modeGSaves.find(s => s.id === stateId);
    if (!state) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Restaurer l'état "${state.name}" ?\nLes données actuelles seront remplacées.`)) {
        restoreModeGData(state.blocks);
        calculerG();
        showNotification(`État "${state.name}" restauré`, 'success');
    }
}

function deleteModeGSave(stateId) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette sauvegarde ?')) {
        modeGSaves = modeGSaves.filter(s => s.id !== stateId);
        localStorage.setItem(MODE_G_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeGSaves));
        displayModeGSaves();
        showNotification('Sauvegarde supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

// ============================================
// MODE G - FONCTIONS AVEC MISE À JOUR AUTOMATIQUE
// ============================================
function addGBlock() {
    const container = document.getElementById("multiGContainer");
    if (!container) return;
    
    const blockId = 'gblock-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
    
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = "ref-block";
    div.id = blockId;
    div.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; width:100%; flex-wrap: wrap;">
            <input type="text" class="bloc-nom-input" value="Nouveau bloc" placeholder="Nom du bloc" style="flex:1; min-width: 200px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
            <label style="color: var(--text-primary); white-space: nowrap;">TVA Globale (%): <input type="number" class="global-tva-input" value="20" style="width:70px;" oninput="autoCalculG()"></label>
            <button onclick="addGLigneToBlock('${blockId}')" class="secondary"><i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Ligne</button>
            <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
        </div>
        <div class="ligneGContainer" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:10px; margin-top:15px;">
            <div class="ligne-tva" id="ligne-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}" style="display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:center; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                <input type="text" class="libelleG" placeholder="Libellé" value="Produit" style="flex:1; min-width: 150px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                <input type="number" class="valeurG" placeholder="Montant" value="100" style="width:110px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                <select class="typeG" style="width:90px;" onchange="autoCalculG()">
                    <option value="HT">HT</option><option value="TTC" selected>TTC</option>
                </select>
                <input type="number" class="tvaIndividuelleG" placeholder="TVA (%)" value="20" style="width:80px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn" style="padding: 5px 10px;"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(div);
    autoCalculG();
    saveAllData();
    
    setTimeout(enhanceNumberInputs, 0);
}

function addGLigneToBlock(blockId) {
    const block = document.getElementById(blockId);
    if (!block) return;
    
    const container = block.querySelector('.ligneGContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const ligneId = 'ligne-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
    
    const ligneDiv = document.createElement("div");
    ligneDiv.className = "ligne-tva";
    ligneDiv.id = ligneId;
    ligneDiv.style.cssText = "display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:center; flex-wrap: wrap;";
    ligneDiv.innerHTML = `
        <input type="text" class="libelleG" placeholder="Libellé" style="flex:1; min-width: 150px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
        <input type="number" class="valeurG" placeholder="Montant" value="100" style="width:110px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
        <select class="typeG" style="width:90px;" onchange="autoCalculG()">
            <option value="HT">HT</option><option value="TTC" selected>TTC</option>
        </select>
        <input type="number" class="tvaIndividuelleG" placeholder="TVA (%)" value="20" style="width:80px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
        <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn" style="padding: 5px 10px;"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(ligneDiv);
    autoCalculG();
    saveAllData();
    
    setTimeout(enhanceNumberInputs, 0);
}

function autoCalculG() {
    calculerG();
    saveAllData();
}

function calculerG() {
    const resultDiv = document.getElementById("resultG");
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiGContainer .ref-block');
    
    if (!resultDiv) return;
    
    if (blocks.length === 0) {
        resultDiv.innerHTML = '<p style="color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Ajoutez un bloc TVA pour commencer</p>';
        return;
    }
    
    let globalHTML = "";
    
    blocks.forEach((block, blockIndex) => {
        const blocNom = block.querySelector('.bloc-nom-input')?.value || `Bloc ${blockIndex + 1}`;
        const globalTVA = parseFloat(block.querySelector('.global-tva-input')?.value) || 20;
        
        const lignes = block.querySelectorAll('.ligne-tva');
        let totalHT = 0;
        let totalTVA = 0;
        let totalTTC = 0;
        
        let tableHTML = `
            <div style="margin: 20px 0; padding: 20px; background: var(--bg-tertiary); border-radius: 10px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color);">
                <h4 style="color: var(--text-primary); margin: 0 0 15px 0;">${blocNom} (TVA par défaut: ${globalTVA}%)</h4>
                <table style="width:100%; margin-top:10px; border-collapse: collapse;">
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">Libellé</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">Montant</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">Type</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">TVA</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">HT</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">TVA (€)</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">TTC</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
        `;
        
        lignes.forEach(ligne => {
            const libelle = ligne.querySelector('.libelleG')?.value || "";
            const valeur = parseFloat(ligne.querySelector('.valeurG')?.value) || 0;
            const type = ligne.querySelector('.typeG')?.value || 'HT';
            const tva = parseFloat(ligne.querySelector('.tvaIndividuelleG')?.value) || globalTVA;
            
            let ht, ttc, tvaEur;
            
            if (type === "HT") {
                ht = valeur;
                tvaEur = ht * (tva / 100);
                ttc = ht + tvaEur;
            } else {
                ttc = valeur;
                ht = ttc / (1 + (tva / 100));
                tvaEur = ttc - ht;
            }
            
            totalHT += ht;
            totalTVA += tvaEur;
            totalTTC += ttc;
            
            tableHTML += `
                <tr style="border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-light);">
                    <td style="padding: 10px;">${libelle}</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${valeur.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: center;">${type}</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${tva.toFixed(1)}%</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${ht.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${tvaEur.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${ttc.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                </tr>
            `;
        });
        
        tableHTML += `
                    </tbody>
                    <tfoot style="font-weight:bold; background:rgba(52, 152, 219, 0.1);">
                        <tr>
                            <td colspan="4" style="padding: 10px; border-top: 2px solid var(--border-color);">TOTAUX</td>
                            <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right; border-top: 2px solid var(--border-color);">${totalHT.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                            <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right; border-top: 2px solid var(--border-color);">${totalTVA.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                            <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right; border-top: 2px solid var(--border-color);">${totalTTC.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                        </tr>
                    </tfoot>
                </table>
            </div>
        `;
        
        globalHTML += tableHTML;
    });
    
    resultDiv.innerHTML = globalHTML;
    saveAllData();
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS DES TABLEAUX
// ============================================
function loadTablesFromMemory() {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(TABLE_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (saved) {
        try {
            const data = JSON.parse(saved);
            tables = data.tables || [];
            currentTableId = data.currentTableId || null;
            tableCounter = data.tableCounter || 1;
            numberingType = data.numberingType || 'numbers';
        } catch(e) {
            console.error('Erreur chargement tableaux:', e);
        }
    }
}

function saveTables() {
    const data = {
        tables: tables,
        currentTableId: currentTableId,
        tableCounter: tableCounter,
        numberingType: numberingType,
        saveDate: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    localStorage.setItem(TABLE_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
}

function createDefaultTable() {
    const today = formatDate(new Date());
    const yesterday = formatDate(new Date(Date.now() - 86400000));
    const twoDaysAgo = formatDate(new Date(Date.now() - 172800000));
    
    const newTable = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: 'Tableau 1',
        columns: [
            { id: Date.now() + 1, name: 'Catégorie', type: 'text' },
            { id: Date.now() + 2, name: 'Valeur', type: 'number' }
        ],
        rows: [
            { id: Date.now() + 3, date: today, cells: [
                { columnId: Date.now() + 1, value: 'Exemple 1' },
                { columnId: Date.now() + 2, value: '100' }
            ]},
            { id: Date.now() + 4, date: yesterday, cells: [
                { columnId: Date.now() + 1, value: 'Exemple 2' },
                { columnId: Date.now() + 2, value: '200' }
            ]},
            { id: Date.now() + 5, date: twoDaysAgo, cells: [
                { columnId: Date.now() + 1, value: 'Exemple 3' },
                { columnId: Date.now() + 2, value: '300' }
            ]}
        ],
        columnWidths: {}
    };
    
    tables.push(newTable);
    currentTableId = newTable.id;
    tableCounter = 2;
    saveTables();
}

function formatDate(date) {
    const day = date.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0');
    const month = (date.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0');
    const year = date.getFullYear();
    return `${day}/${month}/${year}`;
}
