function renderTableTabs() {
    const tabsContainer = document.getElementById('tableTabs');
    const controls = document.getElementById('tableControls');
    
    if (!tabsContainer) return;
    
    if (tables.length === 0) {
        tabsContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center w-full" style="padding: 20px; color: var(--text-muted);"><i class="fas fa-table fa-3x" style="margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><p>Aucun tableau créé</p></div>';
        if (controls) controls.style.display = 'none';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    tables.forEach(table => {
        const isActive = table.id === currentTableId;
        html += `<div class="table-tab ${isActive ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="switchTable(${table.id})">
            ${table.name}
            <span class="table-tab-close" onclick="deleteTable(${table.id}, event)">×</span>
        </div>`;
    });
    
    tabsContainer.innerHTML = html;
    if (controls) controls.style.display = 'flex';
}

function switchTable(tableId) {
    currentTableId = tableId;
    renderTableTabs();
    renderCurrentTable();
}

function renderCurrentTable() {
    const container = document.getElementById('tablePreviewContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (!currentTableId) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center" style="padding: 50px; color: var(--text-muted);"><i class="fas fa-table fa-3x" style="margin-bottom: 20px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><h4>Aucun tableau sélectionné</h4><p>Créez ou sélectionnez un tableau pour commencer</p></div>';
        return;
    }
    
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const nameInput = document.getElementById('tableNameInput');
    if (nameInput) nameInput.value = table.name;
    
    let html = `<table class="table-preview-table" id="main-data-table"><thead><tr>`;
    
    html += `<th onclick="toggleNumberingType()" title="Cliquer pour changer le type">
        <div class="column-header">
            <span>#</span>
            <button class="menu-btn" onclick="showColumnMenu(0, event)">
                <i class="fas fa-ellipsis-v"></i>
            </button>
        </div>
    </th>`;
    
    table.columns.forEach((column, index) => {
        const width = table.columnWidths && table.columnWidths[column.id] ? table.columnWidths[column.id] + 'px' : '120px';
        html += `<th data-column-id="${column.id}" data-column-index="${index}" 
                     style="width: ${width}; min-width: ${width}; max-width: ${width};"
                     draggable="true" 
                     ondragstart="onColumnDragStart(event, ${column.id}, ${index})"
                     ondragover="onColumnDragOver(event)"
                     ondragend="onColumnDragEnd(event)"
                     ondragenter="onColumnDragEnter(event)"
                     ondragleave="onColumnDragLeave(event)">
            <div class="column-header">
                <input type="text" class="column-name-input" 
                       value="${column.name}" 
                       onchange="updateColumnName(${column.id}, this.value)"
                       placeholder="Nom">
                <button class="menu-btn" onclick="showColumnMenu(${column.id}, event)">
                    <i class="fas fa-ellipsis-v"></i>
                </button>
            </div>
            <div class="resize-handle" onmousedown="startResize(event, ${column.id})"></div>
        </th>`;
    });
    
    html += `</tr></thead><tbody>`;
    
    table.rows.forEach((row, rowIndex) => {
        html += `<tr data-row-id="${row.id}" data-row-index="${rowIndex}"
                     draggable="true"
                     ondragstart="onRowDragStart(event, ${row.id}, ${rowIndex})"
                     ondragover="onRowDragOver(event)"
                     ondragend="onRowDragEnd(event)"
                     ondragenter="onRowDragEnter(event)"
                     ondragleave="onRowDragLeave(event)">`;
        
        html += `<td><div class="cell-container">`;
        
        if (numberingType === 'dates') {
            const dateValue = row.date || formatDate(new Date());
            if (!row.date) row.date = dateValue;
            html += `<div class="date-display" onclick="openDatepicker(${row.id})" data-row-id="${row.id}">
                        <i class="fas fa-calendar-alt"></i> ${dateValue}
                    </div>`;
        } else {
            html += `<span class="number-display">${rowIndex + 1}</span>`;
        }
        
        html += `<button class="cell-menu-btn" onclick="showRowMenu(${row.id}, event)">
                    <i class="fas fa-ellipsis-v"></i>
                </button>
            </div></td>`;
        
        table.columns.forEach(column => {
            const cell = row.cells.find(c => c.columnId === column.id);
            const cellValue = cell ? cell.value : '';
            const inputType = column.type === 'number' ? 'number' : 'text';
            
            html += `<td>
                <input type="${inputType}" class="table-cell-input"
                       value="${cellValue}"
                       onchange="updateCellValue(${row.id}, ${column.id}, this.value)"
                       placeholder="${column.type === 'number' ? '0' : '...'}">
            </td>`;
        });
        
        html += `</tr>`;
    });
    
    html += `<tr>
        <td colspan="${table.columns.length + 1}" style="text-align: center; padding: 15px;">
            <button onclick="addTableRow()" class="secondary-btn w-full">
                <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Ajouter une ligne
            </button>
        </td>
    </tr></tbody></table>`;
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
    setTimeout(enhanceNumberInputs, 0);
}

function startResize(event, columnId) {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    
    const th = event.target.closest('th');
    if (!th) return;
    
    currentResizeColumn = {
        id: columnId,
        element: th,
        startWidth: th.offsetWidth
    };
    
    currentResizeStartX = event.clientX;
    currentResizeStartWidth = th.offsetWidth;
    
    document.body.classList.add('resizing-cursor');
    th.classList.add('resizing');
    event.target.classList.add('resizing');
}

function onResize(event) {
    if (!currentResizeColumn) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    
    const dx = event.clientX - currentResizeStartX;
    let newWidth = Math.max(80, currentResizeStartWidth + dx);
    
    currentResizeColumn.element.style.width = newWidth + 'px';
    currentResizeColumn.element.style.minWidth = newWidth + 'px';
    currentResizeColumn.element.style.maxWidth = newWidth + 'px';
}

function stopResize() {
    if (currentResizeColumn) {
        document.body.classList.remove('resizing-cursor');
        currentResizeColumn.element.classList.remove('resizing');
        
        const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
        if (table) {
            if (!table.columnWidths) table.columnWidths = {};
            table.columnWidths[currentResizeColumn.id] = currentResizeColumn.element.offsetWidth;
            saveTables();
        }
        currentResizeColumn = null;
    }
}

function onColumnDragStart(event, columnId, index) {
    if (event.target.classList.contains('column-name-input') || 
        event.target.classList.contains('menu-btn') ||
        event.target.classList.contains('resize-handle')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        return false;
    }
    
    dragSourceType = 'column';
    dragSourceId = columnId;
    dragSourceIndex = index;
    
    event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
    event.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', columnId);
    event.target.classList.add('dragging');
}

function onColumnDragOver(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
}

function onColumnDragEnter(event) {
    const th = event.target.closest('th');
    if (!th || !th.dataset.columnId) return;
    
    th.classList.remove('drop-target-left', 'drop-target-right');
    
    const rect = th.getBoundingClientRect();
    const mouseX = event.clientX;
    const threshold = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
    
    if (mouseX < threshold) {
        th.classList.add('drop-target-left');
    } else {
        th.classList.add('drop-target-right');
    }
}

function onColumnDragLeave(event) {
    const th = event.target.closest('th');
    if (th) th.classList.remove('drop-target-left', 'drop-target-right');
}

function onColumnDragEnd() {
    document.querySelectorAll('th').forEach(th => {
        th.classList.remove('dragging', 'drop-target-left', 'drop-target-right');
    });
    dragSourceType = null;
    dragSourceId = null;
    dragSourceIndex = null;
}

function onRowDragStart(event, rowId, index) {
    if (event.target.classList.contains('cell-menu-btn') || 
        event.target.classList.contains('date-display')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        return false;
    }
    
    dragSourceType = 'row';
    dragSourceId = rowId;
    dragSourceIndex = index;
    
    event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
    event.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', rowId);
    event.target.closest('tr').classList.add('dragging');
}

function onRowDragOver(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
}

function onRowDragEnter(event) {
    const tr = event.target.closest('tr');
    if (!tr || !tr.dataset.rowId) return;
    
    tr.classList.remove('drop-target-above', 'drop-target-below');
    
    const rect = tr.getBoundingClientRect();
    const mouseY = event.clientY;
    const threshold = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
    
    if (mouseY < threshold) {
        tr.classList.add('drop-target-above');
    } else {
        tr.classList.add('drop-target-below');
    }
}

function onRowDragLeave(event) {
    const tr = event.target.closest('tr');
    if (tr) tr.classList.remove('drop-target-above', 'drop-target-below');
}

function onRowDragEnd() {
    document.querySelectorAll('tr').forEach(tr => {
        tr.classList.remove('dragging', 'drop-target-above', 'drop-target-below');
    });
    dragSourceType = null;
    dragSourceId = null;
    dragSourceIndex = null;
}

function handleDrop(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    
    if (!currentTableId || !dragSourceType) return;
    
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    if (dragSourceType === 'column') {
        const targetTh = event.target.closest('th');
        if (!targetTh || !targetTh.dataset.columnId) return;
        
        const targetColumnId = parseInt(targetTh.dataset.columnId);
        const sourceColumnId = dragSourceId;
        
        if (sourceColumnId === targetColumnId) return;
        
        const sourceIndex = table.columns.findIndex(col => col.id === sourceColumnId);
        const targetIndex = table.columns.findIndex(col => col.id === targetColumnId);
        
        if (sourceIndex === -1 || targetIndex === -1) return;
        
        const rect = targetTh.getBoundingClientRect();
        const mouseX = event.clientX;
        let insertIndex = targetIndex;
        
        if (mouseX > rect.left + rect.width / 2) insertIndex = targetIndex + 1;
        
        const [movedColumn] = table.columns.splice(sourceIndex, 1);
        if (sourceIndex < insertIndex) insertIndex--;
        table.columns.splice(insertIndex, 0, movedColumn);
        
        table.rows.forEach(row => {
            const cells = [...row.cells];
            const movedCellIndex = cells.findIndex(cell => cell.columnId === sourceColumnId);
            const [movedCell] = cells.splice(movedCellIndex, 1);
            
            let targetCellIndex = cells.findIndex(cell => cell.columnId === targetColumnId);
            if (insertIndex > targetIndex) targetCellIndex++;
            
            cells.splice(targetCellIndex, 0, movedCell);
            row.cells = cells;
        });
        
        saveTables();
        renderCurrentTable();
        showNotification('Colonne déplacée', 'success');
        
    } else if (dragSourceType === 'row') {
        const targetTr = event.target.closest('tr');
        if (!targetTr || !targetTr.dataset.rowId) return;
        
        const targetRowId = parseInt(targetTr.dataset.rowId);
        const sourceRowId = dragSourceId;
        
        if (sourceRowId === targetRowId) return;
        
        const sourceIndex = table.rows.findIndex(row => row.id === sourceRowId);
        const targetIndex = table.rows.findIndex(row => row.id === targetRowId);
        
        if (sourceIndex === -1 || targetIndex === -1) return;
        
        const rect = targetTr.getBoundingClientRect();
        const mouseY = event.clientY;
        let insertIndex = targetIndex;
        
        if (mouseY > rect.top + rect.height / 2) insertIndex = targetIndex + 1;
        
        const [movedRow] = table.rows.splice(sourceIndex, 1);
        if (sourceIndex < insertIndex) insertIndex--;
        table.rows.splice(insertIndex, 0, movedRow);
        
        saveTables();
        renderCurrentTable();
        showNotification('Ligne déplacée', 'success');
    }
    
    document.querySelectorAll('th, tr').forEach(el => {
        el.classList.remove('drop-target-left', 'drop-target-right', 'drop-target-above', 'drop-target-below');
    });
    
    dragSourceType = null;
    dragSourceId = null;
    dragSourceIndex = null;
}

function openDatepicker(rowId) {
    const dateDisplay = document.querySelector(`.date-display[data-row-id="${rowId}"]`);
    if (!dateDisplay || typeof Datepicker === 'undefined') return;
    
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const row = table.rows.find(r => r.id === rowId);
    if (!row) return;
    
    let currentDate = new Date();
    if (row.date) {
        const parts = row.date.split('/');
        if (parts.length === 3) {
            currentDate = new Date(parseInt(parts[2]), parseInt(parts[1]) - 1, parseInt(parts[0]));
        }
    }
    
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'text';
    input.style.position = 'absolute';
    input.style.opacity = '0';
    input.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    document.body.appendChild(input);
    
    const datepicker = new Datepicker(input, {
        language: 'fr',
        format: 'dd/mm/yyyy',
        autohide: true,
        todayHighlight: true
    });
    
    datepicker.setDate(currentDate);
    datepicker.show();
    
    input.addEventListener('changeDate', function(e) {
        const date = e.date;
        if (date) {
            const formattedDate = formatDate(date);
            row.date = formattedDate;
            saveTables();
            dateDisplay.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-calendar-alt"></i> ${formattedDate}`;
            showNotification(`Date mise à jour: ${formattedDate}`, 'success');
        }
        setTimeout(() => {
            datepicker.destroy();
            document.body.removeChild(input);
        }, 100);
    });
}

function showColumnMenu(columnId, event) {
    event.stopPropagation();
    currentMenuContext = { type: 'column', id: columnId, element: event.currentTarget };
    showContextMenu(event.clientX, event.clientY);
}

function showRowMenu(rowId, event) {
    event.stopPropagation();
    currentMenuContext = { type: 'row', id: rowId, element: event.currentTarget };
    showContextMenu(event.clientX, event.clientY);
}

function showContextMenu(x, y) {
    const contextMenu = document.getElementById('contextMenu');
    if (!contextMenu) return;
    contextMenu.style.left = x + 'px';
    contextMenu.style.top = y + 'px';
    contextMenu.classList.add('show');
}

function handleMenuAction(action) {
    const contextMenu = document.getElementById('contextMenu');
    if (contextMenu) contextMenu.classList.remove('show');
    
    if (!currentTableId || !currentMenuContext.id) return;
    
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    if (currentMenuContext.type === 'column') {
        if (currentMenuContext.id === 0) {
            if (action === 'delete') showNotification('Impossible de supprimer la colonne #', 'warning');
            return;
        }
        if (action === 'duplicate') duplicateColumn(currentMenuContext.id);
        else if (action === 'clear') clearColumn(currentMenuContext.id);
        else if (action === 'delete') deleteColumn(currentMenuContext.id);
    } else if (currentMenuContext.type === 'row') {
        if (action === 'duplicate') duplicateRow(currentMenuContext.id);
        else if (action === 'clear') clearRow(currentMenuContext.id);
        else if (action === 'delete') deleteRow(currentMenuContext.id);
    }
}

function addNumericColumn() {
    const columnName = prompt('Nom de la catégorie numérique :', `Valeur ${getNextColumnNumber()}`);
    if (!columnName) return;
    addTableColumn(columnName, 'number');
}

function addTextColumn() {
    const columnName = prompt('Nom de la catégorie texte :', `Texte ${getNextColumnNumber()}`);
    if (!columnName) return;
    addTableColumn(columnName, 'text');
}

function getNextColumnNumber() {
    if (!currentTableId) return 1;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    return table ? table.columns.length + 1 : 1;
}

function addTableColumn(columnName, type) {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const newColumn = { id: Date.now(), name: columnName, type: type };
    table.columns.push(newColumn);
    
    table.rows.forEach(row => {
        row.cells.push({ columnId: newColumn.id, value: type === 'number' ? '0' : '' });
    });
    
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification(`Catégorie "${columnName}" ajoutée`, 'success');
}

function duplicateColumn(columnId) {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const column = table.columns.find(col => col.id === columnId);
    if (!column) return;
    
    const newColumn = { id: Date.now(), name: `${column.name} (copie)`, type: column.type };
    table.columns.push(newColumn);
    
    table.rows.forEach(row => {
        const originalCell = row.cells.find(c => c.columnId === column.id);
        row.cells.push({
            columnId: newColumn.id,
            value: originalCell ? originalCell.value : (column.type === 'number' ? '0' : '')
        });
    });
    
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification('Colonne dupliquée', 'success');
}

function clearColumn(columnId) {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const column = table.columns.find(col => col.id === columnId);
    if (!column) return;
    
    table.rows.forEach(row => {
        const cell = row.cells.find(c => c.columnId === column.id);
        if (cell) cell.value = column.type === 'number' ? '0' : '';
    });
    
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification('Contenu de la colonne effacé', 'success');
}

function deleteColumn(columnId) {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table || table.columns.length <= 1) {
        showNotification('Un tableau doit avoir au moins une colonne', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    table.columns = table.columns.filter(col => col.id !== columnId);
    table.rows.forEach(row => {
        row.cells = row.cells.filter(cell => cell.columnId !== columnId);
    });
    
    if (table.columnWidths) delete table.columnWidths[columnId];
    
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification('Colonne supprimée', 'success');
}

function updateColumnName(columnId, newName) {
    if (!currentTableId || !newName) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const column = table.columns.find(col => col.id === columnId);
    if (column) {
        column.name = newName;
        saveTables();
        showNotification('Nom de colonne mis à jour', 'success');
    }
}

function addTableRow() {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const todayFormatted = formatDate(new Date());
    const newRow = {
        id: Date.now(),
        date: todayFormatted,
        cells: table.columns.map(column => ({
            columnId: column.id,
            value: column.type === 'number' ? '0' : ''
        }))
    };
    
    table.rows.push(newRow);
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification('Ligne ajoutée', 'success');
}

function duplicateRow(rowId) {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const row = table.rows.find(r => r.id === rowId);
    if (!row) return;
    
    const newRow = {
        id: Date.now(),
        date: formatDate(new Date()),
        cells: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(row.cells))
    };
    
    table.rows.push(newRow);
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification('Ligne dupliquée', 'success');
}

function clearRow(rowId) {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const row = table.rows.find(r => r.id === rowId);
    if (!row) return;
    
    row.cells.forEach(cell => {
        const column = table.columns.find(col => col.id === cell.columnId);
        if (column) cell.value = column.type === 'number' ? '0' : '';
    });
    
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification('Contenu de la ligne effacé', 'success');
}

function deleteRow(rowId) {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table || table.rows.length <= 1) {
        showNotification('Un tableau doit avoir au moins une ligne', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    table.rows = table.rows.filter(r => r.id !== rowId);
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification('Ligne supprimée', 'success');
}

function duplicateCurrentRow() {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table || table.rows.length === 0) return;
    
    const lastRow = table.rows[table.rows.length - 1];
    const newRow = {
        id: Date.now(),
        date: formatDate(new Date()),
        cells: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(lastRow.cells))
    };
    
    table.rows.push(newRow);
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification('Ligne dupliquée', 'success');
}

function updateCellValue(rowId, columnId, newValue) {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    const row = table.rows.find(r => r.id === rowId);
    if (row) {
        const cell = row.cells.find(c => c.columnId === columnId);
        if (cell) {
            cell.value = newValue;
            saveTables();
        }
    }
}

function toggleNumberingType() {
    numberingType = numberingType === 'numbers' ? 'dates' : 'numbers';
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification(`Numérotation changée en : ${numberingType === 'numbers' ? 'Numéros' : 'Dates'}`, 'success');
}

function setNumberingType(type) {
    numberingType = type;
    saveTables();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification(`Numérotation changée en : ${type === 'numbers' ? 'Numéros' : 'Dates'}`, 'success');
}

function renameCurrentTable() {
    if (!currentTableId) return;
    const input = document.getElementById('tableNameInput');
    if (!input) return;
    const newName = input.value.trim();
    if (!newName) {
        showNotification('Le nom ne peut pas être vide', 'error');
        return;
    }
    
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (table) {
        table.name = newName;
        saveTables();
        renderTableTabs();
        showNotification('Tableau renommé', 'success');
    }
}

function deleteTable(tableId, event = null) {
    if (event) event.stopPropagation();
    
    if (tables.length <= 1) {
        showNotification('Vous devez garder au moins un tableau', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const table = tables.find(t => t.id === tableId);
    if (!table) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Supprimer le tableau "${table.name}" ?`)) {
        tables = tables.filter(t => t.id !== tableId);
        if (currentTableId === tableId) {
            currentTableId = tables.length > 0 ? tables[0].id : null;
        }
        
        saveTables();
        renderTableTabs();
        renderCurrentTable();
        showNotification('Tableau supprimé', 'success');
    }
}

function createNewTable() {
    const newTable = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: `Tableau ${tableCounter++}`,
        columns: [
            { id: Date.now() + 1, name: 'Catégorie', type: 'text' },
            { id: Date.now() + 2, name: 'Valeur', type: 'number' }
        ],
        rows: [
            { id: Date.now() + 3, date: formatDate(new Date()), cells: [] }
        ],
        columnWidths: {}
    };
    
    newTable.rows[0].cells = newTable.columns.map(column => ({
        columnId: column.id,
        value: column.type === 'number' ? '0' : ''
    }));
    
    tables.push(newTable);
    currentTableId = newTable.id;
    
    saveTables();
    renderTableTabs();
    renderCurrentTable();
    showNotification(`Nouveau tableau créé: ${newTable.name}`, 'success');
}

function exportTableauData() {
    if (tables.length === 0) {
        showNotification('Aucun tableau à exporter', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const exportData = {
        tables: tables,
        currentTableId: currentTableId,
        tableCounter: tableCounter,
        numberingType: numberingType,
        exportDate: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    
    const dataStr = JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2);
    const dataUri = 'data:application/json;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(dataStr);
    const fileName = `tableaux_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
    
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = dataUri;
    link.download = fileName;
    link.click();
    
    showNotification('Tableaux exportés', 'success');
}

function importTableauData() {
    const input = document.getElementById('tableauFileInput');
    if (!input) return;
    
    input.onchange = function(event) {
        const file = event.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = function(e) {
            try {
                const importedData = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
                if (!importedData.tables || !Array.isArray(importedData.tables)) {
                    throw new Error('Format invalide');
                }
                
                if (confirmWithSetting(`Importer ${importedData.tables.length} tableau(x) ?\nLes données actuelles seront remplacées.`)) {
                    tables = importedData.tables;
                    currentTableId = importedData.currentTableId || (tables.length > 0 ? tables[0].id : null);
                    tableCounter = importedData.tableCounter || 1;
                    numberingType = importedData.numberingType || 'numbers';
                    
                    saveTables();
                    renderTableTabs();
                    renderCurrentTable();
                    showNotification(`${tables.length} tableau(x) importé(s)`, 'success');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                showNotification('Erreur lors de l\'importation', 'error');
            }
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
        event.target.value = '';
    };
    input.click();
}

function saveTableState() {
    const stateName = prompt('Nom de l\'état :', `Tableau_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}_${new Date().toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')}`);
    if (!stateName) return;
    
    const currentTable = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
    if (!currentTable) {
        showNotification('Aucun tableau sélectionné', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const state = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: stateName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        tableData: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(currentTable)),
        numberingType: numberingType
    };
    
    modeTableSaves.unshift(state);
    if (modeTableSaves.length > 10) modeTableSaves = modeTableSaves.slice(0, 10);
    
    localStorage.setItem(MODE_TABLE_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeTableSaves));
    displayTableSavesList();
    showNotification(`État "${stateName}" sauvegardé pour le tableau "${currentTable.name}"`, 'success');
}

function restoreTableState(stateId) {
    const state = modeTableSaves.find(s => s.id === stateId);
    if (!state) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting(`Restaurer l'état "${state.name}" ?\nLe tableau actuel sera remplacé.`)) {
        const targetTable = tables.find(t => t.id === currentTableId);
        if (targetTable) {
            Object.assign(targetTable, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.tableData)));
        } else {
            tables.push(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.tableData)));
            currentTableId = tables[tables.length - 1].id;
        }
        
        numberingType = state.numberingType || 'numbers';
        
        saveTables();
        renderTableTabs();
        renderCurrentTable();
        showNotification(`État "${state.name}" restauré`, 'success');
    }
}

function deleteTableSave(stateId) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer cette sauvegarde de tableau ?')) {
        modeTableSaves = modeTableSaves.filter(s => s.id !== stateId);
        localStorage.setItem(MODE_TABLE_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(modeTableSaves));
        displayTableSavesList();
        showNotification('Sauvegarde supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

function displayTableSavesList() {
    const container = document.getElementById('tableSavesList');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (modeTableSaves.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Aucune sauvegarde pour les tableaux</div>';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    modeTableSaves.forEach(save => {
        const date = new Date(save.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
        const tableName = save.tableData ? save.tableData.name : 'Tableau';
        html += `
            <div class="save-item">
                <div class="save-info">
                    <div class="save-name">${save.name}</div>
                    <div class="save-date">${date} - ${tableName}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="save-actions">
                    <button onclick="restoreTableState(${save.id})" class="secondary save-btn-small">
                        <i class="fas fa-undo"></i>
                    </button>
                    <button onclick="deleteTableSave(${save.id})" class="delete-btn save-btn-small">
                        <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

// ============================================
// TABLEAU DE BORD - ACCUEIL UTILE
// ============================================
function updateDashboard() {
    updateRecentCalculations();
    updateDashboardStats();
}

function updateRecentCalculations() {
    const container = document.getElementById('recentCalculations');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const allRecent = [
        ...recentCalculations,
        ...calcHistory.slice(0, 3).map(item => ({
            type: 'calculatrice',
            title: `Calculatrice: ${item.expression || ''}`,
            value: item.result,
            timestamp: item.timestamp,
            mode: 'modeCalculator'
        })),
        ...conversionHistory.slice(0, 2).map(item => ({
            type: 'devise',
            title: `Conversion: ${item.from.amount} ${item.from.currency} → ${item.to.currency}`,
            value: item.to.amount,
            timestamp: item.date,
            mode: 'modeH'
        }))
    ].sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp || b.date) - new Date(a.timestamp || a.date)).slice(0, 5);
    
    if (allRecent.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px 20px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <i class="fas fa-history fa-2x" style="margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.5;"></i>
                <p>Aucun calcul récent</p>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    allRecent.forEach(item => {
        const date = item.timestamp || item.date || 'Récemment';
        const value = typeof item.value === 'number' ? item.value.toFixed(2) : item.value;
        
        html += `
            <div class="recent-item" onclick="showMode('${item.mode || 'modeCalculator'}')">
                <div>
                    <div style="font-weight: bold;">${item.title}</div>
                    <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${date}</div>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <span class="recent-type">${item.type}</span>
                    <div style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--accent-color); text-align: right; margin-top: 5px;">${value}</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function updateDashboardStats() {
    const today = new Date().toDateString();
    const todayCalculations = calcHistory.filter(item => 
        item.timestamp && new Date(item.timestamp).toDateString() === today
    ).length;
    
    const statCalculs = document.getElementById('statCalculs');
    const statConversions = document.getElementById('statConversions');
    const statGraphs = document.getElementById('statGraphs');
    const statBlocs = document.getElementById('statBlocs');
    
    if (statCalculs) statCalculs.textContent = todayCalculations;
    if (statConversions) statConversions.textContent = conversionHistory.length;
    if (statGraphs) statGraphs.textContent = (chartD ? 1 : 0) + (chartF ? 1 : 0);
    if (statBlocs) statBlocs.textContent = cBlocks.length + budgetCategories.length + notes.length + tables.length;
}

function addRecentCalculation(type, title, value) {
    const calculation = {
        type: type,
        title: title,
        value: value,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleString('fr-FR')
    };
    
    recentCalculations.unshift(calculation);
    if (recentCalculations.length > 20) recentCalculations = recentCalculations.slice(0, 20);
    
    localStorage.setItem(RECENT_CALCULATIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(recentCalculations));
    updateRecentCalculations();
}

function clearRecentCalculations() {
    if (recentCalculations.length === 0) return;
    
    if (confirmWithSetting('Effacer tous les calculs récents ?')) {
        recentCalculations = [];
        localStorage.setItem(RECENT_CALCULATIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify([]));
        updateRecentCalculations();
        showNotification('Calculs récents effacés', 'success');
    }
}

function quickCalculation(type) {
    switch(type) {
        case 'percentage':
            const value = prompt('Entrez la valeur :', '100');
            const percentage = prompt('Entrez le pourcentage :', '20');
            if (value && percentage) {
                const result = (parseFloat(value) * parseFloat(percentage)) / 100;
                showMode('modeCalculator');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const inputField = document.getElementById('calc-input');
                    if (inputField) {
                        inputField.value = `${value}*${percentage}/100`;
                        updatePreview();
                        performExecution();
                    }
                    addRecentCalculation('pourcentage', `Calcul de ${percentage}% de ${value}`, result);
                }, 100);
            }
            break;
            
        case 'vat':
            const ht = prompt('Montant HT :', '100');
            const tva = prompt('Taux TVA (%) :', '20');
            if (ht && tva) {
                const ttc = parseFloat(ht) * (1 + parseFloat(tva)/100);
                showMode('modeG');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    addRecentCalculation('tva', `TVA: ${ht} HT → ${ttc.toFixed(2)} TTC (${tva}%)`, ttc);
                }, 100);
            }
            break;
            
        case 'currency':
            const amount = prompt('Montant à convertir :', '100');
            if (amount) {
                showMode('modeH');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const fromAmount = document.getElementById('fromAmount');
                    if (fromAmount) fromAmount.value = amount;
                    convertCurrency();
                    const toAmount = document.getElementById('toAmount');
                    addRecentCalculation('devise', `Conversion: ${amount} EUR → USD`, 
                        parseFloat(toAmount?.value || 0));
                }, 100);
            }
            break;
    }
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS DES PARAMÈTRES
// ============================================
function loadSettings() {
    const defaultSettings = {
        autoSave: true,
        notifications: true,
        confirmations: true,
        decimalPlaces: 2,
        defaultCurrency: 'EUR',
        autoRound: true,
        defaultTheme: 'light'
    };
    
    const savedSettings = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY) || '{}');
    settings = { ...defaultSettings, ...savedSettings };
    
    const autoSave = document.getElementById('autoSave');
    const notifications = document.getElementById('notifications');
    const confirmations = document.getElementById('confirmations');
    const decimalPlaces = document.getElementById('decimalPlaces');
    const defaultCurrency = document.getElementById('defaultCurrency');
    const autoRound = document.getElementById('autoRound');
    const defaultTheme = document.getElementById('defaultTheme');
    
    if (autoSave) autoSave.checked = settings.autoSave;
    if (notifications) notifications.checked = settings.notifications;
    if (confirmations) confirmations.checked = settings.confirmations;
    if (decimalPlaces) decimalPlaces.value = settings.decimalPlaces;
    if (defaultCurrency) defaultCurrency.value = settings.defaultCurrency;
    if (autoRound) autoRound.checked = settings.autoRound;
    if (defaultTheme) defaultTheme.value = settings.defaultTheme;
}

function saveSettings() {
    const autoSave = document.getElementById('autoSave');
    const notifications = document.getElementById('notifications');
    const confirmations = document.getElementById('confirmations');
    const decimalPlaces = document.getElementById('decimalPlaces');
    const defaultCurrency = document.getElementById('defaultCurrency');
    const autoRound = document.getElementById('autoRound');
    const defaultTheme = document.getElementById('defaultTheme');
    
    const newSettings = {
        autoSave: autoSave ? autoSave.checked : true,
        notifications: notifications ? notifications.checked : true,
        confirmations: confirmations ? confirmations.checked : true,
        decimalPlaces: decimalPlaces ? parseInt(decimalPlaces.value) : 2,
        defaultCurrency: defaultCurrency ? defaultCurrency.value : 'EUR',
        autoRound: autoRound ? autoRound.checked : true,
        defaultTheme: defaultTheme ? defaultTheme.value : 'light'
    };
    
    settings = newSettings;
    
    localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(settings));
    
    showNotification('Paramètres enregistrés', 'success');
    
    // Appliquer immédiatement les paramètres qui nécessitent une action
    if (settings.defaultTheme === 'dark') {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
        currentTheme = 'dark';
    } else if (settings.defaultTheme === 'light') {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
        currentTheme = 'light';
    } else if (settings.defaultTheme === 'auto') {
        const prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
        document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light');
        currentTheme = prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
    }
    
    const themeIcon = document.querySelector('#themeIcon');
    if (themeIcon) {
        themeIcon.className = currentTheme === 'light' ? 'fas fa-moon' : 'fas fa-sun';
    }
}

function resetSettings() {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Réinitialiser tous les paramètres aux valeurs par défaut ?')) {
        localStorage.removeItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
        
        settings = {
            autoSave: true,
            notifications: true,
            confirmations: true,
            decimalPlaces: 2,
            defaultCurrency: 'EUR',
            autoRound: true,
            defaultTheme: 'light'
        };
        
        loadSettings();
        
        // Appliquer le thème par défaut
        document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
        currentTheme = 'light';
        const themeIcon = document.querySelector('#themeIcon');
        if (themeIcon) themeIcon.className = 'fas fa-moon';
        
        showNotification('Paramètres réinitialisés', 'success');
    }
}

function showSettings() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('settingsModal');
    if (modal) {
        modal.classList.remove('hidden');
        loadSettings();
        displayShortcuts();
    }
}

function closeSettings() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('settingsModal');
    if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
}

// ============================================
// FONCTION DE CONFIRMATION AVEC PARAMÈTRE
// ============================================
function confirmWithSetting(message) {
    if (settings && settings.confirmations === false) {
        return true;
    }
    return confirm(message);
}

// ============================================
// GESTIONNAIRE DE RACCOURCIS CLAVIER AMÉLIORÉ
// ============================================
function initKeyboardShortcuts() {
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(event) {
        // Ne pas déclencher si on est dans un champ de saisie
        if (event.target.tagName === 'INPUT' || event.target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || event.target.tagName === 'SELECT') {
            return;
        }
        
        const pressedKey = formatShortcut(event);
        
        keyboardShortcuts.forEach(shortcut => {
            if (shortcut.key === pressedKey) {
                event.preventDefault();
                showMode(shortcut.mode);
                showNotification(`Raccourci: ${shortcut.key} → ${shortcut.description}`, 'info');
            }
        });
    });
}

function formatShortcut(event) {
    const parts = [];
    if (event.ctrlKey) parts.push('Ctrl');
    if (event.altKey) parts.push('Alt');
    if (event.shiftKey) parts.push('Shift');
    if (event.metaKey) parts.push('Meta');
    
    let key = event.key;
    
    // Normaliser les touches spéciales
    if (key === ' ') key = 'Space';
    else if (key === 'Control' || key === 'Alt' || key === 'Shift' || key === 'Meta') {
        // Ignorer les touches modificatrices seules
        return '';
    } else if (key.length === 1) {
        key = key.toUpperCase();
    } else {
        key = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
    }
    
    parts.push(key);
    return parts.join('+');
}

function addShortcut() {
    const keyInput = document.getElementById('shortcutKey');
    const modeSelect = document.getElementById('shortcutMode');
    
    if (!keyInput || !modeSelect) return;
    
    const key = keyInput.value.trim();
    const mode = modeSelect.value;
    
    if (!key) {
        showNotification('Veuillez saisir un raccourci clavier', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    // Vérifier si le raccourci existe déjà
    if (keyboardShortcuts.some(s => s.key === key)) {
        showNotification('Ce raccourci est déjà utilisé', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const shortcut = {
        id: Date.now(),
        key: key,
        mode: mode,
        description: `Ouvrir ${document.querySelector(`option[value="${mode}"]`)?.textContent || mode}`
    };
    
    keyboardShortcuts.push(shortcut);
    localStorage.setItem(SHORTCUTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(keyboardShortcuts));
    
    keyInput.value = '';
    displayShortcuts();
    showNotification('Raccourci ajouté', 'success');
}

function deleteShortcut(id) {
    if (confirmWithSetting('Supprimer ce raccourci ?')) {
        keyboardShortcuts = keyboardShortcuts.filter(s => s.id !== id);
        localStorage.setItem(SHORTCUTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(keyboardShortcuts));
        displayShortcuts();
        showNotification('Raccourci supprimé', 'success');
    }
}

function displayShortcuts() {
    const container = document.getElementById('shortcutsList');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (keyboardShortcuts.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<p style="color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Aucun raccourci personnalisé</p>';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    keyboardShortcuts.forEach(shortcut => {
        html += `
            <div class="shortcut-item">
                <div>
                    <span class="shortcut-key">${shortcut.key}</span>
                    <span style="margin-left: 10px;">${shortcut.description}</span>
                    <div class="shortcut-meta">Mode: ${shortcut.mode}</div>
                </div>
                <button onclick="deleteShortcut(${shortcut.id})" class="delete-btn" style="padding: 5px 10px;">
                    <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                </button>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

// SAUVEGARDE ET CHARGEMENT PRINCIPAUX
// ============================================
function saveAllData() {
    try {
        const appData = {
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            version: '16.0',
            notes: notes,
            settings: settings,
            tables: { tables, currentTableId, tableCounter, numberingType },
            conversionHistory: conversionHistory,
            recentCalculations: recentCalculations,
            calcHistory: calcHistory,
            cBlocks: cBlocks,
            cBlockCounter: cBlockCounter,
            budgetCategories: budgetCategories,
            budgetCatCount: budgetCatCount,
            dDates: dDates,
            dSeries: dSeries,
            modeASaves: modeASaves,
            modeCSaves: modeCSaves,
            modeBSaves: modeBSaves,
            modeDSaves: modeDSaves,
            modeFSaves: modeFSaves,
            modeGSaves: modeGSaves,
            modeTableSaves: modeTableSaves,
            keyboardShortcuts: keyboardShortcuts
        };
        
        localStorage.setItem(APP_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(appData));
        console.log('💾 Données principales sauvegardées');
        return appData;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Erreur sauvegarde:', error);
        return null;
    }
}

function loadAllData() {
    try {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem(APP_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (saved) {
            const data = JSON.parse(saved);
            
            if (data.notes) notes = data.notes;
            if (data.settings) settings = { ...settings, ...data.settings };
            if (data.tables) {
                tables = data.tables.tables || [];
                currentTableId = data.tables.currentTableId || null;
                tableCounter = data.tables.tableCounter || 1;
                numberingType = data.tables.numberingType || 'numbers';
            }
            if (data.conversionHistory) conversionHistory = data.conversionHistory;
            if (data.recentCalculations) recentCalculations = data.recentCalculations;
            if (data.calcHistory) calcHistory = data.calcHistory;
            if (data.cBlocks) cBlocks = data.cBlocks;
            if (data.cBlockCounter) cBlockCounter = data.cBlockCounter;
            if (data.budgetCategories) budgetCategories = data.budgetCategories;
            if (data.budgetCatCount) budgetCatCount = data.budgetCatCount;
            if (data.dDates) dDates = data.dDates;
            if (data.dSeries) dSeries = data.dSeries;
            if (data.modeASaves) modeASaves = data.modeASaves;
            if (data.modeCSaves) modeCSaves = data.modeCSaves;
            if (data.modeBSaves) modeBSaves = data.modeBSaves;
            if (data.modeDSaves) modeDSaves = data.modeDSaves;
            if (data.modeFSaves) modeFSaves = data.modeFSaves;
            if (data.modeGSaves) modeGSaves = data.modeGSaves;
            if (data.modeTableSaves) modeTableSaves = data.modeTableSaves;
            if (data.keyboardShortcuts) keyboardShortcuts = data.keyboardShortcuts;
            
            console.log('✅ Données chargées');
            return true;
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Erreur chargement:', error);
    }
    
    return false;
}

function initDefaultData() {
    if (cBlocks.length === 0) addCBlock();
    if (budgetCategories.length === 0) addBudgetCategory();
    if (dDates.length === 0) {
        dDates.push({ id: Date.now(), date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0] });
        dDates.push({ id: Date.now() + 1, date: new Date(Date.now() + 86400000).toISOString().split('T')[0] });
        dSeries.push({
            id: Date.now() + 2,
            name: "Série 1",
            values: {},
            color: "#" + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16)
        });
    }
    
    const fBlocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    if (fBlocks.length === 0) {
        addFBlock();
        addFBlock();
    }
    
    if (calcHistory.length === 0) {
        calcHistory = [
            {
                id: Date.now() - 1000,
                expression: "2+2",
                result: 4,
                timestamp: new Date().toLocaleString('fr-FR')
            },
            {
                id: Date.now() - 2000,
                expression: "sin(pi/2)",
                result: 1,
                timestamp: new Date().toLocaleString('fr-FR')
            }
        ];
        localStorage.setItem(CALCULATOR_HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(calcHistory));
    }
}

function initModes() {
    if (cBlocks.length === 0) addCBlock();
    if (budgetCategories.length === 0) addBudgetCategory();
    if (dDates.length === 0) {
        addDDate();
        addDDate();
    }
    if (dSeries.length === 0) addDSeries();
    
    const fBlocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    if (fBlocks.length === 0) {
        addFBlock();
        addFBlock();
    }
    
    const gBlocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiGContainer .ref-block');
    if (gBlocks.length === 0) addGBlock();
    
    const aBlocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiAContainer .ref-block');
    if (aBlocks.length === 0) addABlock();
}

// ============================================
// THÈME
// ============================================
function toggleTheme() {
    currentTheme = currentTheme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light';
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', currentTheme);
    localStorage.setItem('calculator_theme', currentTheme);
    
    const themeIcon = document.querySelector('#themeIcon');
    if (themeIcon) themeIcon.className = currentTheme === 'light' ? 'fas fa-moon' : 'fas fa-sun';
    
    showNotification(`Thème ${currentTheme === 'light' ? 'clair' : 'sombre'} activé`, 'info');
}

function loadTheme() {
    const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('calculator_theme') || settings.defaultTheme || 'light';
    
    if (savedTheme === 'auto') {
        const prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
        currentTheme = prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
    } else {
        currentTheme = savedTheme;
    }
    
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', currentTheme);
    
    const themeIcon = document.querySelector('#themeIcon');
    if (themeIcon) themeIcon.className = currentTheme === 'light' ? 'fas fa-moon' : 'fas fa-sun';
}

// ============================================
// FONCTION DE NOTIFICATION
// ============================================
function showNotification(message, type = 'success', duration = 4000) {
    if (!settings || !settings.notifications) return;
    
    const container = document.getElementById('notificationContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const notification = document.createElement('div');
    notification.className = `notification ${type}`;
    
    const icon = type === 'success' ? 'check-circle' :
                 type === 'error' ? 'exclamation-circle' :
                 type === 'warning' ? 'exclamation-triangle' : 'info-circle';
    
    notification.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-${icon}"></i> ${message}`;
    
    container.appendChild(notification);
    
    setTimeout(() => {
        notification.style.animation = 'slideOut 0.3s ease';
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (container.contains(notification)) {
                container.removeChild(notification);
            }
        }, 300);
    }, duration);
    
    const style = document.querySelector('#notification-animation') || document.createElement('style');
    style.id = 'notification-animation';
    style.textContent = `
        @keyframes slideOut {
            from { transform: translateX(0); opacity: 1; }
            to { transform: translateX(100%); opacity: 0; }
        }
    `;
    if (!document.querySelector('#notification-animation')) {
        document.head.appendChild(style);
    }
}

// ============================================
// FONCTION DE FORMATAGE DES NOMBRES
// ============================================
function formatNumberWithSettings(value) {
    const decimalPlaces = (settings && settings.decimalPlaces) || 2;
    const rounded = (settings && settings.autoRound) ? parseFloat(value).toFixed(decimalPlaces) : value;
    return parseFloat(rounded);
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS MANQUANTES (Mode A, etc.)
// ============================================
function addABlock() {
    const container = document.getElementById('multiAContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'ref-block';
    div.innerHTML = `
        <input type="number" class="refA" placeholder="Référence" value="100" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculA()">
        <input type="text" class="valsA" placeholder="Valeurs (séparées par espaces)" value="80 120 100" style="flex:1;" oninput="autoCalculA()">
        <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
    `;
    container.appendChild(div);
    autoCalculA();
}

function removeNode(btn) {
    const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
    if (block) {
        block.remove();
        autoCalculA();
    }
}

function autoCalculA() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiAContainer .ref-block');
    const resultDiv = document.getElementById('resultA');
    if (!resultDiv) return;
    
    if (blocks.length === 0) {
        resultDiv.innerHTML = '';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    blocks.forEach((block, index) => {
        const ref = parseFloat(block.querySelector('.refA')?.value) || 0;
        const valsStr = block.querySelector('.valsA')?.value || '';
        const vals = valsStr.split(/\s+/).map(v => parseFloat(v)).filter(v => !isNaN(v));
        
        const lessCount = vals.filter(v => v < ref).length;
        const greaterCount = vals.filter(v => v > ref).length;
        const equalCount = vals.filter(v => v === ref).length;
        
        html += `
            <div style="background: var(--bg-tertiary); border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 8px; padding: 15px; margin: 10px 0;">
                <h4 style="margin: 0 0 10px 0;">Bloc ${index + 1}</h4>
                <p>Référence: ${ref}</p>
                <p>Valeurs: ${vals.join(', ')}</p>
                <p>Inférieurs: ${lessCount} | Égaux: ${equalCount} | Supérieurs: ${greaterCount}</p>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    resultDiv.innerHTML = html;
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    console.log('🚀 Calculateur Global Pro + Tableaux v16 - Calculatrice Scientifique');
    
    loadSettings();
    loadTheme();
    loadAllData();
    loadNotes();
    initModes();
    initDefaultData();
    loadExchangeRates();
    
    // Initialisation de l'historique de la calculatrice
    const savedCalcHistory = localStorage.getItem(CALCULATOR_HISTORY_KEY);
    if (savedCalcHistory) {
        try {
            calcHistory = JSON.parse(savedCalcHistory);
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Erreur chargement historique calculatrice:', e);
        }
    }
    updateCalcHistoryDisplay();
    updateDashboard();
    
    loadTablesFromMemory();
    if (tables.length === 0) createDefaultTable();
    
    updateCalculations();
    
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', stopResize);
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onResize);
    document.addEventListener('drop', handleDrop);
    document.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => e.preventDefault());
    document.addEventListener('click', function(event) {
        const contextMenu = document.getElementById('contextMenu');
        if (contextMenu && contextMenu.classList.contains('show') && !contextMenu.contains(event.target)) {
            contextMenu.classList.remove('show');
        }
    });
    
    // Événements pour la calculatrice
    const calcInput = document.getElementById('calc-input');
    if (calcInput) {
        calcInput.addEventListener('input', updatePreview);
        calcInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                e.preventDefault();
                performExecution();
            }
        });
    }
    
    enhanceNumberInputs();
    
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    if (sidebar) {
        if (sidebarCollapsed) {
            sidebar.classList.add('collapsed');
            const toggleBtn = document.querySelector('.toggle-btn i');
            if (toggleBtn) toggleBtn.className = 'fas fa-chevron-right';
        }
    }
    
    loadTheme();
    
    const homeItem = document.querySelector('.menu-item[onclick*="home"]');
    if (homeItem) homeItem.classList.add('active');
    
    // Initialiser les raccourcis clavier
    initKeyboardShortcuts();
    
    // Démarrer la sauvegarde automatique
    initAutoSave();
    
    showNotification('Calculateur Global Pro + Tableaux v16 prêt !', 'success');
});
